import sys 
def find_max_val(n, x, y): 
	ans = -sys.maxsize 
	for k in range(n + 1): 
		if (k % x == y): 
			ans = max(ans, k) 
	return (ans if (ans >= 0 and
					ans <= n) else -1)

def average_Even(n) : 
    if (n% 2!= 0) : 
        return ("Invalid Input") 
        return -1  
    sm = 0
    count = 0
    while (n>= 2) : 
        count = count+1
        sm = sm+n 
        n = n-2
    return sm // count

import math 
def get_Pos_Of_Right_most_Set_Bit(n): 
    return int(math.log2(n&-n)+1)   
def set_Right_most_Unset_Bit(n): 
    if (n == 0): 
        return 1
    if ((n & (n + 1)) == 0):     
        return n 
    pos = get_Pos_Of_Right_most_Set_Bit(~n)      
    return ((1 << (pos - 1)) | n)

    if (num1 >= num2) and (num1 >= num3):
       lnum = num1
    elif (num2 >= num1) and (num2 >= num3):
       lnum = num2
    else:
       lnum = num3
    return lnum

def smallest_Divisor(n): 
    if (n % 2 == 0): 
        return 2; 
    i = 3;  
    while (i*i <= n): 
        if (n % i == 0): 
            return i; 
        i += 2; 
    return n;

def sum_Square(n) : 
    i = 1 
    while i*i <= n : 
        j = 1
        while (j*j <= n) : 
            if (i*i+j*j == n) : 
                return True
            j = j+1
        i = i+1     
    return False

def count_Char(str,x): 
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(str)):  
        if (str[i] == x) : 
            count += 1
    n = 10
    repititions = n // len(str)  
    count = count * repititions  
    l = n % len(str)  
    for i in range(l): 
        if (str[i] == x):  
            count += 1
    return count

def sum_Of_Primes(n): 
    prime = [True] * (n + 1)  
    p = 2
    while p * p <= n: 
        if prime[p] == True:  
            i = p * 2
            while i <= n: 
                prime[i] = False
                i += p 
        p += 1    
    sum = 0
    for i in range (2,n + 1): 
        if(prime[i]): 
            sum += i 
    return sum

def recur_gcd(a, b):
	low = min(a, b)
	high = max(a, b)
	if low == 0:
		return high
	elif low == 1:
		return 1
	else:
		return recur_gcd(low, high%low)

def min_jumps(arr, n):
	jumps = [0 for i in range(n)]
	if (n == 0) or (arr[0] == 0):
		return float('inf')
	jumps[0] = 0
	for i in range(1, n):
		jumps[i] = float('inf')
		for j in range(i):
			if (i <= j + arr[j]) and (jumps[j] != float('inf')):
				jumps[i] = min(jumps[i], jumps[j] + 1)
				break
	return jumps[n-1]

from itertools import groupby 
def group_element(test_list):
  res = dict()
  for key, val in groupby(sorted(test_list, key = lambda ele: ele[1]), key = lambda ele: ele[1]):
    res[key] = [ele[0] for ele in val] 
  return (res)

def equilibrium_index(arr):
  total_sum = sum(arr)
  left_sum=0
  for i, num in enumerate(arr):
    total_sum -= num
    if left_sum == total_sum:
      return i
    left_sum += num
  return -1

def find_ind(key, i, n, 
			k, arr):
	ind = -1
	start = i + 1
	end = n - 1;
	while (start < end):
		mid = int(start +
				(end - start) / 2)
		if (arr[mid] - key <= k):
			ind = mid
			start = mid + 1
		else:
			end = mid
	return ind
def removals(arr, n, k):
	ans = n - 1
	arr.sort()
	for i in range(0, n):
		j = find_ind(arr[i], i, 
					n, k, arr)
		if (j != -1):
			ans = min(ans, n -
						(j - i + 1))
	return ans

    return 1

    return 1 / n + (harmonic_sum(n - 1))

def is_subset(arr1, m, arr2, n): 
	hashset = set() 
	for i in range(0, m): 
		hashset.add(arr1[i]) 
	for i in range(0, n): 
		if arr2[i] in hashset: 
			continue
		else: 
			return False
	return True

def count_Set_Bits(n) :  
    n += 1; 
    powerOf2 = 2;   
    cnt = n // 2;  
    while (powerOf2 <= n) : 
        totalPairs = n // powerOf2;  
        cnt += (totalPairs // 2) * powerOf2;  
        if (totalPairs & 1) : 
            cnt += (n % powerOf2) 
        else : 
            cnt += 0
        powerOf2 <<= 1;    
    return cnt;

from collections import defaultdict 
def get_unique(test_list):
  res = defaultdict(list)
  for sub in test_list:
    res[sub[1]].append(sub[0])
  res = dict(res)
  res_dict = dict()
  for key in res:
    res_dict[key] = len(list(set(res[key])))
  return (str(res_dict))

def product_Equal(n): 
    if n < 10: 
        return False
    prodOdd = 1; prodEven = 1
    while n > 0: 
        digit = n % 10
        prodOdd *= digit 
        n = n//10
        if n == 0: 
            break; 
        digit = n % 10
        prodEven *= digit 
        n = n//10
    if prodOdd == prodEven: 
        return True
    return False

def count_Fac(n):  
    m = n 
    count = 0
    i = 2
    while((i * i) <= m): 
        total = 0
        while (n % i == 0): 
            n /= i 
            total += 1 
        temp = 0
        j = 1
        while((temp + j) <= total): 
            temp += j 
            count += 1
            j += 1 
        i += 1
    if (n != 1): 
        count += 1 
    return count

import math
import sys
def sd_calc(data):
    n = len(data)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0
    mean, sd = avg_calc(data), 0.0
    for el in data:
        sd += (float(el) - mean)**2
    sd = math.sqrt(sd / float(n-1))
    return sd
def avg_calc(ls):
    n, mean = len(ls), 0.0
    if n <= 1:
        return ls[0]
    for el in ls:
        mean = mean + float(el)
    mean = mean / float(n)
    return mean

M = 100
def maxAverageOfPath(cost, N): 
	dp = [[0 for i in range(N + 1)] for j in range(N + 1)] 
	dp[0][0] = cost[0][0] 
	for i in range(1, N): 
		dp[i][0] = dp[i - 1][0] + cost[i][0] 
	for j in range(1, N): 
		dp[0][j] = dp[0][j - 1] + cost[0][j] 
	for i in range(1, N): 
		for j in range(1, N): 
			dp[i][j] = max(dp[i - 1][j], 
						dp[i][j - 1]) + cost[i][j] 
	return dp[N - 1][N - 1] / (2 * N - 1)

def find_first_occurrence(A, x):
    (left, right) = (0, len(A) - 1)
    result = -1
    while left <= right:
        mid = (left + right) // 2
        if x == A[mid]:
            result = mid
            right = mid - 1
        elif x < A[mid]:
            right = mid - 1
        else:
            left = mid + 1
    return result

def sum_Of_Subarray_Prod(arr,n):
    ans = 0
    res = 0
    i = n - 1
    while (i >= 0):
        incr = arr[i]*(1 + res)
        ans += incr
        res = incr
        i -= 1
    return (ans)

def set_middle_bits(n):  
    n |= n >> 1; 
    n |= n >> 2; 
    n |= n >> 4; 
    n |= n >> 8; 
    n |= n >> 16;  
    return (n >> 1) ^ 1
def toggle_middle_bits(n): 
    if (n == 1): 
        return 1
    return n ^ set_middle_bits(n)

    return 0

        return None

def lcs_of_three(X, Y, Z, m, n, o): 
	L = [[[0 for i in range(o+1)] for j in range(n+1)] 
		for k in range(m+1)] 
	for i in range(m+1): 
		for j in range(n+1): 
			for k in range(o+1): 
				if (i == 0 or j == 0 or k == 0): 
					L[i][j][k] = 0
				elif (X[i-1] == Y[j-1] and
					X[i-1] == Z[k-1]): 
					L[i][j][k] = L[i-1][j-1][k-1] + 1
				else: 
					L[i][j][k] = max(max(L[i-1][j][k], 
					L[i][j-1][k]), 
									L[i][j][k-1]) 
	return L[m][n][o]

def check_min_heap(arr, i):
    if 2 * i + 2 > len(arr):
        return True
    left_child = (arr[i] <= arr[2 * i + 1]) and check_min_heap(arr, 2 * i + 1)
    right_child = (2 * i + 2 == len(arr)) or (arr[i] <= arr[2 * i + 2] 
                                      and check_min_heap(arr, 2 * i + 2))
    return left_child and right_child

def jacobsthal_num(n): 
	dp = [0] * (n + 1) 
	dp[0] = 0
	dp[1] = 1
	for i in range(2, n+1): 
		dp[i] = dp[i - 1] + 2 * dp[i - 2] 
	return dp[n]

def second_smallest(numbers):
  if (len(numbers)<2):
    return
  if ((len(numbers)==2)  and (numbers[0] == numbers[1]) ):
    return
  dup_items = set()
  uniq_items = []
  for x in numbers:
    if x not in dup_items:
      uniq_items.append(x)
      dup_items.add(x)
  uniq_items.sort()    
  return  uniq_items[1]

    for i in range(len(l1) - 1):

def check_Odd_Parity(x): 
    parity = 0
    while (x != 0): 
        x = x & (x - 1) 
        parity += 1
    if (parity % 2 == 1): 
        return True
    else: 
        return False

    sm = 0

from collections import deque
def check_expression(exp):
    if len(exp) & 1:
        return False
    stack = deque()
    for ch in exp:
        if ch == '(' or ch == '{' or ch == '[':
            stack.append(ch)
        if ch == ')' or ch == '}' or ch == ']':
            if not stack:
                return False
            top = stack.pop()
            if (top == '(' and ch != ')') or (top == '{' and ch != '}' or (top == '[' and ch != ']')):
                return False
    return not stack

def count_vowels(test_str):
  res = 0
  vow_list = ['a', 'e', 'i', 'o', 'u']
  for idx in range(1, len(test_str) - 1):
    if test_str[idx] not in vow_list and (test_str[idx - 1] in vow_list or test_str[idx + 1] in vow_list):
      res += 1
  if test_str[0] not in vow_list and test_str[1] in vow_list:
    res += 1
  if test_str[-1] not in vow_list and test_str[-2] in vow_list:
    res += 1
  return (res)

def find_Sum(arr,n): 
    arr.sort() 
    sum = arr[0] 
    for i in range(0,n-1): 
        if (arr[i] != arr[i+1]): 
            sum = sum + arr[i+1]   
    return sum

import math 
def count_Divisors(n) : 
    count = 0
    for i in range(1, (int)(math.sqrt(n)) + 2) : 
        if (n % i == 0) : 
            if( n // i == i) : 
                count = count + 1
            else : 
                count = count + 2
    if (count % 2 == 0) : 
        return ("Even") 
    else : 
        return ("Odd")

def Odd_Length_Sum(arr):
    Sum = 0
    l = len(arr)
    for i in range(l):
        Sum += ((((i + 1) *(l - i) + 1) // 2) * arr[i])
    return Sum

def rgb_to_hsv(r, g, b):
    r, g, b = r/255.0, g/255.0, b/255.0
    mx = max(r, g, b)
    mn = min(r, g, b)
    df = mx-mn
    if mx == mn:
        h = 0
    elif mx == r:
        h = (60 * ((g-b)/df) + 360) % 360
    elif mx == g:
        h = (60 * ((b-r)/df) + 120) % 360
    elif mx == b:
        h = (60 * ((r-g)/df) + 240) % 360
    if mx == 0:
        s = 0
    else:
        s = (df/mx)*100
    v = mx*100
    return h, s, v

def mul_even_odd(list1):
    first_even = next((el for el in list1 if el%2==0),-1)
    first_odd = next((el for el in list1 if el%2!=0),-1)
    return (first_even*first_odd)

def last(arr,x,n):
    low = 0
    high = n - 1
    res = -1  
    while (low <= high):
        mid = (low + high) // 2 
        if arr[mid] > x:
            high = mid - 1
        elif arr[mid] < x:
            low = mid + 1
        else:
            res = mid
            low = mid + 1
    return res

def is_Perfect_Square(n) :
    i = 1
    while (i * i<= n):
        if ((n % i == 0) and (n / i == i)):
            return True     
        i = i + 1
    return False

  res = 0
  for idx in range(0, len(test_str)):
    if test_str[idx].isupper():

      res = cnt

  if test_str[len(test_str) - 1].isupper():

def sort_by_dnf(arr, n):
  low=0
  mid=0
  high=n-1
  while mid <= high:
    if arr[mid] == 0:
      arr[low], arr[mid] = arr[mid], arr[low]
      low = low + 1
      mid = mid + 1
    elif arr[mid] == 1:
      mid = mid + 1
    else:
      arr[mid], arr[high] = arr[high], arr[mid]
      high = high - 1
  return arr

def count_duplic(lists):
    element = []
    frequency = []
    if not lists:
        return element
    running_count = 1
    for i in range(len(lists)-1):
        if lists[i] == lists[i+1]:
            running_count += 1
        else:
            frequency.append(running_count)
            element.append(lists[i])
            running_count = 1
    frequency.append(running_count)
    element.append(lists[i+1])
    return element,frequency

import re
def pass_validity(p):
 x = True
 while x:  
    if (len(p)<6 or len(p)>12):
        break
    elif not re.search("[a-z]",p):
        break
    elif not re.search("[0-9]",p):
        break
    elif not re.search("[A-Z]",p):
        break
    elif not re.search("[$#@]",p):
        break
    elif re.search("\s",p):
        break
    else:
        return True
        x=False
        break

 if x:
    return False

import re 
def check_substring(string, sample) : 
  if (sample in string): 
      y = "\A" + sample 
      x = re.search(y, string)

          return ("string starts with the given substring") 
      else : 
          return ("string doesnt start with the given substring")

      return ("entered string isnt a substring")

def check_Type_Of_Triangle(a,b,c): 
    sqa = pow(a,2) 
    sqb = pow(b,2) 
    sqc = pow(c,2) 
    if (sqa == sqa + sqb or sqb == sqa + sqc or sqc == sqa + sqb): 
        return ("Right-angled Triangle") 
    elif (sqa > sqc + sqb or sqb > sqa + sqc or sqc > sqa + sqb): 
        return ("Obtuse-angled Triangle") 
    else: 
        return ("Acute-angled Triangle")

def count_alpha_dig_spl(string):
  alphabets=digits = special = 0
  for i in range(len(string)):
    if(string[i].isalpha()):
        alphabets = alphabets + 1
    elif(string[i].isdigit()):
        digits = digits + 1
    else:
        special = special + 1
  return (alphabets,digits,special)

	dict = Counter(input) 
	value = sorted(dict.values(), reverse=True)

		if val == second_large:

def generate_matrix(n):
        if n<=0:
            return [] 
        matrix=[row[:] for row in [[0]*n]*n]        
        row_st=0
        row_ed=n-1        
        col_st=0
        col_ed=n-1
        current=1        
        while (True):
            if current>n*n:
                break
            for c in range (col_st, col_ed+1):
                matrix[row_st][c]=current
                current+=1
            row_st+=1
            for r in range (row_st, row_ed+1):
                matrix[r][col_ed]=current
                current+=1
            col_ed-=1
            for c in range (col_ed, col_st-1, -1):
                matrix[row_ed][c]=current
                current+=1
            row_ed-=1
            for r in range (row_ed, row_st-1, -1):
                matrix[r][col_st]=current
                current+=1
            col_st+=1
        return matrix

from sys import maxsize 
def max_sub_array_sum(a,size): 
	max_so_far = -maxsize - 1
	max_ending_here = 0
	start = 0
	end = 0
	s = 0
	for i in range(0,size): 
		max_ending_here += a[i] 
		if max_so_far < max_ending_here: 
			max_so_far = max_ending_here 
			start = s 
			end = i 
		if max_ending_here < 0: 
			max_ending_here = 0
			s = i+1
	return (end - start + 1)

def cube_Sum(n): 
    sum = 0   
    for i in range(0,n) : 
        sum += (2*i+1)*(2*i+1)*(2*i+1) 
    return sum

def min_Swaps(s1,s2) :  
    c0 = 0; c1 = 0;  
    for i in range(len(s1)) :  
        if (s1[i] == '0' and s2[i] == '1') : 
            c0 += 1;    
        elif (s1[i] == '1' and s2[i] == '0') : 
            c1 += 1;  
    result = c0 // 2 + c1 // 2;  
    if (c0 % 2 == 0 and c1 % 2 == 0) : 
        return result;  
    elif ((c0 + c1) % 2 == 0) : 
        return result + 2;  
    else : 
        return -1;

def sort_tuple(tup): 
	n = len(tup) 
	for i in range(n): 
		for j in range(n-i-1): 
			if tup[j][0] > tup[j + 1][0]: 
				tup[j], tup[j + 1] = tup[j + 1], tup[j] 
	return tup

def get_inv_count(arr, n): 
	inv_count = 0
	for i in range(n): 
		for j in range(i + 1, n):

				inv_count += 1
	return inv_count

def get_odd_occurence(arr, arr_size):
  for i in range(0, arr_size):
    count = 0
    for j in range(0, arr_size):
      if arr[i] == arr[j]:
        count += 1
    if (count % 2 != 0):
      return arr[i]
  return -1

import heapq
def nth_super_ugly_number(n, primes):
    uglies = [1]
    def gen(prime):
        for ugly in uglies:
            yield ugly * prime
    merged = heapq.merge(*map(gen, primes))
    while len(uglies) < n:
        ugly = next(merged)
        if ugly != uglies[-1]:
            uglies.append(ugly)
    return uglies[-1]

def get_Number(n, k): 
    arr = [0] * n; 
    i = 0; 
    odd = 1; 
    while (odd <= n):   
        arr[i] = odd; 
        i += 1; 
        odd += 2;
    even = 2; 
    while (even <= n): 
        arr[i] = even; 
        i += 1;
        even += 2; 
    return arr[k - 1];

import math 
def find_Digits(n): 
    if (n < 0): 
        return 0;
    if (n <= 1): 
        return 1; 
    x = ((n * math.log10(n / math.e) + math.log10(2 * math.pi * n) /2.0)); 
    return math.floor(x) + 1;

def find_platform(arr, dep, n): 
    arr.sort() 
    dep.sort() 
    plat_needed = 1
    result = 1
    i = 1
    j = 0
    while (i < n and j < n): 
        if (arr[i] <= dep[j]):           
            plat_needed+= 1
            i+= 1
        elif (arr[i] > dep[j]):           
            plat_needed-= 1
            j+= 1
        if (plat_needed > result):  
            result = plat_needed           
    return result

def Sum(N): 
    SumOfPrimeDivisors = [0]*(N + 1)   
    for i in range(2,N + 1) : 
        if (SumOfPrimeDivisors[i] == 0) : 
            for j in range(i,N + 1,i) : 
                SumOfPrimeDivisors[j] += i           
    return SumOfPrimeDivisors[N]

    if(a != 0 and b != 0 and c != 0 and (a + b + c)== 180): 
        if((a + b)>= c or (b + c)>= a or (a + c)>= b): 
            return True 
        else:
            return False
    else:
        return False

import math
def sum_of_odd_Factors(n): 
    res = 1
    while n % 2 == 0: 
        n = n // 2 
    for i in range(3,int(math.sqrt(n) + 1)): 
        count = 0
        curr_sum = 1
        curr_term = 1
        while n % i == 0: 
            count+=1 
            n = n // i 
            curr_term *= i 
            curr_sum += curr_term    
        res *= curr_sum  
    if n >= 2: 
        res *= (1 + n) 
    return res

def check_Even_Parity(x): 
    parity = 0
    while (x != 0): 
        x = x & (x - 1) 
        parity += 1
    if (parity % 2 == 0): 
        return True
    else: 
        return False

def find_Min_Swaps(arr,n) : 
    noOfZeroes = [0] * n 
    count = 0 
    noOfZeroes[n - 1] = 1 - arr[n - 1] 
    for i in range(n-2,-1,-1) : 
        noOfZeroes[i] = noOfZeroes[i + 1] 
        if (arr[i] == 0) : 
            noOfZeroes[i] = noOfZeroes[i] + 1
    for i in range(0,n) : 
        if (arr[i] == 1) : 
            count = count + noOfZeroes[i] 
    return count

from itertools import combinations
def sub_lists(my_list):
	subs = []
	for i in range(0, len(my_list)+1):
	  temp = [list(x) for x in combinations(my_list, i)]
	  if len(temp)>0:
	    subs.extend(temp)
	return subs

def find_longest_conseq_subseq(arr, n): 
	ans = 0
	count = 0
	arr.sort() 
	v = [] 
	v.append(arr[0]) 
	for i in range(1, n): 
		if (arr[i] != arr[i - 1]): 
			v.append(arr[i]) 
	for i in range(len(v)): 
		if (i > 0 and v[i] == v[i - 1] + 1): 
			count += 1
		else: 
			count = 1
		ans = max(ans, count) 
	return ans

def lcm(x, y):
   if x > y:
       z = x
   else:
       z = y
   while(True):
       if((z % x == 0) and (z % y == 0)):
           lcm = z
           break
       z += 1
   return lcm

def all_Bits_Set_In_The_Given_Range(n,l,r): 
    num = ((1 << r) - 1) ^ ((1 << (l - 1)) - 1) 
    new_num = n & num 
    if (num == new_num): 
        return True
    return False

def is_Isomorphic(str1,str2):          
    dict_str1 = {}
    dict_str2 = {}
    for i, value in enumerate(str1):
        dict_str1[value] = dict_str1.get(value,[]) + [i]        
    for j, value in enumerate(str2):
        dict_str2[value] = dict_str2.get(value,[]) + [j]
    if sorted(dict_str1.values()) == sorted(dict_str2.values()):
        return True
    else:
        return False

def max_sum_subseq(A):
    n = len(A)
    if n == 1:
        return A[0]
    look_up = [None] * n
    look_up[0] = A[0]
    look_up[1] = max(A[0], A[1])
    for i in range(2, n):
        look_up[i] = max(look_up[i - 1], look_up[i - 2] + A[i])
        look_up[i] = max(look_up[i], A[i])
    return look_up[n - 1]

def smallest_multiple(n):
    if (n<=2):
      return n
    i = n * 2
    factors = [number  for number in range(n, 1, -1) if number * 2 > n]
    while True:
        for a in factors:
            if i % a != 0:
                i += n
                break
            if (a == factors[-1] and i % a == 0):
                return i

def count_Unset_Bits(n) :  
    cnt = 0;  
    for i in range(1,n + 1) : 
        temp = i;  
        while (temp) :  
            if (temp % 2 == 0) : 
                cnt += 1;  
            temp = temp // 2;  
    return cnt;

def maximum_product(nums):
    import heapq
    a, b = heapq.nlargest(3, nums), heapq.nsmallest(2, nums)
    return max(a[0] * a[1] * a[2], a[0] * b[0] * b[1])

def binomial_coeff(n, k): 
	C = [[0 for j in range(k + 1)] 
			for i in range(n + 1)] 
	for i in range(0, n + 1): 
		for j in range(0, min(i, k) + 1): 
			if (j == 0 or j == i): 
				C[i][j] = 1
			else: 
				C[i][j] = (C[i - 1][j - 1] 
							+ C[i - 1][j]) 
	return C[n][k] 
def lobb_num(n, m): 
	return (((2 * m + 1) *
		binomial_coeff(2 * n, m + n)) 
					/ (m + n + 1))

def find_triplet_array(A, arr_size, sum): 
	for i in range( 0, arr_size-2): 
		for j in range(i + 1, arr_size-1): 
			for k in range(j + 1, arr_size): 
				if A[i] + A[j] + A[k] == sum: 
					return  A[i],A[j],A[k] 
					return True
	return False

def coin_change(S, m, n): 
    table = [[0 for x in range(m)] for x in range(n+1)] 
    for i in range(m): 
        table[0][i] = 1
    for i in range(1, n+1): 
        for j in range(m): 
            x = table[i - S[j]][j] if i-S[j] >= 0 else 0
            y = table[i][j-1] if j >= 1 else 0 
            table[i][j] = x + y   
    return table[n][m-1]

def max_product(arr): 
    arr_len = len(arr) 
    if (arr_len < 2): 
        return None     
    x = arr[0]; y = arr[1]    
    for i in range(0, arr_len): 
        for j in range(i + 1, arr_len): 
            if (arr[i] * arr[j] > x * y): 
                x = arr[i]; y = arr[j] 
    return x,y

def super_seq(X, Y, m, n):
	if (not m):
		return n
	if (not n):
		return m
	if (X[m - 1] == Y[n - 1]):
		return 1 + super_seq(X, Y, m - 1, n - 1)
	return 1 + min(super_seq(X, Y, m - 1, n),	super_seq(X, Y, m, n - 1))

def binomial_coeffi(n, k): 
	if (k == 0 or k == n): 
		return 1
	return (binomial_coeffi(n - 1, k - 1) 
		+ binomial_coeffi(n - 1, k)) 
def rencontres_number(n, m): 
	if (n == 0 and m == 0): 
		return 1
	if (n == 1 and m == 0): 
		return 0
	if (m == 0): 
		return ((n - 1) * (rencontres_number(n - 1, 0)+ rencontres_number(n - 2, 0))) 
	return (binomial_coeffi(n, m) * rencontres_number(n - m, 0))

	def __init__(self, data): 
		self.data = data 
		self.left = None

def max_height(node): 
	if node is None: 
		return 0 ; 
	else : 
		left_height = max_height(node.left) 
		right_height = max_height(node.right) 
		if (left_height > right_height): 
			return left_height+1
		else: 
			return right_height+1

import sys 

def find_closet(A, B, C, p, q, r): 
	diff = sys.maxsize 
	res_i = 0
	res_j = 0
	res_k = 0
	i = 0
	j = 0
	k = 0
	while(i < p and j < q and k < r): 
		minimum = min(A[i], min(B[j], C[k])) 
		maximum = max(A[i], max(B[j], C[k])); 
		if maximum-minimum < diff: 
			res_i = i 
			res_j = j 
			res_k = k 
			diff = maximum - minimum; 
		if diff == 0: 
			break
		if A[i] == minimum: 
			i = i+1
		elif B[j] == minimum: 
			j = j+1
		else: 
			k = k+1
	return A[res_i],B[res_j],C[res_k]

def sorted_models(models):

 return sorted_models

def heap_sort(arr):
    heapify(arr)  
    end = len(arr) - 1
    while end > 0:
        arr[end], arr[0] = arr[0], arr[end]
        shift_down(arr, 0, end - 1)
        end -= 1
    return arr

def heapify(arr):
    start = len(arr) // 2
    while start >= 0:
        shift_down(arr, start, len(arr) - 1)
        start -= 1
def shift_down(arr, start, end):
    root = start
    while root * 2 + 1 <= end:
        child = root * 2 + 1
        if child + 1 <= end and arr[child] < arr[child + 1]:
            child += 1
        if child <= end and arr[root] < arr[child]:
            arr[root], arr[child] = arr[child], arr[root]
            root = child
        else:
            return

def count_digs(tup):
  return sum([len(str(ele)) for ele in tup ]) 
def sort_list(test_list):
  test_list.sort(key = count_digs)
  return (str(test_list))

def chinese_zodiac(year):
 if (year - 2000) % 12 == 0:
     sign = 'Dragon'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 1:
     sign = 'Snake'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 2:
     sign = 'Horse'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 3:
     sign = 'sheep'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 4:
     sign = 'Monkey'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 5:
     sign = 'Rooster'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 6:
     sign = 'Dog'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 7:
     sign = 'Pig'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 8:
     sign = 'Rat'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 9:
     sign = 'Ox'
 elif (year - 2000) % 12 == 10:
     sign = 'Tiger'
 else:
     sign = 'Hare'
 return sign

def max_similar_indices(test_list1, test_list2):
  res = [(max(x[0], y[0]), max(x[1], y[1]))
   for x, y in zip(test_list1, test_list2)]
  return (res)

def nCr_mod_p(n, r, p): 
	if (r > n- r): 
		r = n - r 
	C = [0 for i in range(r + 1)] 
	C[0] = 1 
	for i in range(1, n + 1): 
		for j in range(min(i, r), 0, -1): 
			C[j] = (C[j] + C[j-1]) % p 
	return C[r]

def subset(ar, n): 
    res = 0
    ar.sort() 
    for i in range(0, n) : 
        count = 1
        for i in range(n - 1): 
            if ar[i] == ar[i + 1]: 
                count+=1
            else: 
                break 
        res = max(res, count)  
    return res

def profit_amount(actual_cost,sale_amount): 
 if(actual_cost > sale_amount):
    amount = actual_cost - sale_amount
    return amount
 else:
    return None

def is_abundant(n):
    fctrsum = sum([fctr for fctr in range(1, n) if n % fctr == 0])
    return fctrsum > n

import math
def get_First_Set_Bit_Pos(n):
     return math.log2(n&-n)+1

def int_to_roman( num):
        val = [1000, 900, 500, 400,100, 90, 50, 40,10, 9, 5, 4,1]
        syb = ["M", "CM", "D", "CD","C", "XC", "L", "XL","X", "IX", "V", "IV","I"]
        roman_num = ''
        i = 0
        while  num > 0:
            for _ in range(num // val[i]):
                roman_num += syb[i]
                num -= val[i]
            i += 1
        return roman_num

def get_noOfways(n):
    if (n == 0):
        return 0;
    if (n == 1):
        return 1; 
    return get_noOfways(n - 1) + get_noOfways(n - 2);

def roman_to_int(s):
        rom_val = {'I': 1, 'V': 5, 'X': 10, 'L': 50, 'C': 100, 'D': 500, 'M': 1000}
        int_val = 0
        for i in range(len(s)):
            if i > 0 and rom_val[s[i]] > rom_val[s[i - 1]]:
                int_val += rom_val[s[i]] - 2 * rom_val[s[i - 1]]
            else:
                int_val += rom_val[s[i]]
        return int_val

def sum_Natural(n): 
    sum = (n * (n + 1)) 
    return int(sum) 
def sum_Even(l,r): 
    return (sum_Natural(int(r / 2)) - sum_Natural(int((l - 1) / 2)))

def discriminant_value(x,y,z):
    discriminant = (y**2) - (4*x*z)
    if discriminant > 0:
        return ("Two solutions",discriminant)
    elif discriminant == 0:
        return ("one solution",discriminant)
    elif discriminant < 0:
        return ("no real solution",discriminant)

def word_len(s): 
    s = s.split(' ')   
    for word in s:    
        if len(word)%2==0: 
            return True  
        else:
          return False

def camel_to_snake(text):
        import re
        str1 = re.sub('(.)([A-Z][a-z]+)', r'\1_\2', text)
        return re.sub('([a-z0-9])([A-Z])', r'\1_\2', str1).lower()

def remove_empty(tuple1): #L = [(), (), ('',), ('a', 'b'), ('a', 'b', 'c'), ('d')]
   tuple1 = [t for t in tuple1 if t]
   return tuple1

    return ("not accepted")

def floor_Max(A,B,N):
    x = min(B - 1,N)
    return (A*x) // B

def join_tuples(test_list):
  res = []
  for sub in test_list:
    if res and res[-1][0] == sub[0]:
      res[-1].extend(sub[1:])
    else:
      res.append([ele for ele in sub])
  res = list(map(tuple, res))
  return (res)

def min_of_two( x, y ):
    if x < y:
        return x
    return y

def maximum_segments(n, a, b, c) : 
	dp = [-1] * (n + 10) 
	dp[0] = 0
	for i in range(0, n) : 
		if (dp[i] != -1) : 
			if(i + a <= n ): 
				dp[i + a] = max(dp[i] + 1, 
							dp[i + a]) 
			if(i + b <= n ): 
				dp[i + b] = max(dp[i] + 1, 
							dp[i + b]) 
			if(i + c <= n ): 
				dp[i + c] = max(dp[i] + 1, 
							dp[i + c]) 
	return dp[n]

def concatenate_nested(test_tup1, test_tup2):
  res = test_tup1 + test_tup2
  return (res)

def left_rotate(s,d):
    tmp = s[d : ] + s[0 : d]
    return tmp

def min_sum_path(A): 
	memo = [None] * len(A) 
	n = len(A) - 1
	for i in range(len(A[n])): 
		memo[i] = A[n][i] 
	for i in range(len(A) - 2, -1,-1): 
		for j in range( len(A[i])): 
			memo[j] = A[i][j] + min(memo[j], 
									memo[j + 1]) 
	return memo[0]

class SimpleNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super(SimpleNN, self).__init__()
        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(10, 5)
        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(5, 1)
    def forward(self, x):
        x = torch.relu(self.fc1(x))
        x = self.fc2(x)
        return x

class AdvancedNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super(AdvancedNN, self).__init__()
        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(10, 128)
        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 64)
        self.fc3 = nn.Linear(64, 32)
        self.fc4 = nn.Linear(32, 1)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(0.2)
    
    def forward(self, x):
        x = torch.relu(self.fc1(x))
        x = self.dropout(x)
        x = torch.relu(self.fc2(x))
        x = self.dropout(x)
        x = torch.relu(self.fc3(x))
        x = self.fc4(x)
        return x

class NLPNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, vocab_size, embed_dim):
        super(NLPNet, self).__init__()
        self.embedding = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, embed_dim)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(embed_dim, 1)
    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.embedding(x)
        x = self.fc(x)
        return x

class PID:
    def __init__(self, Kp, Ki, Kd):
        self.Kp = Kp
        self.Ki = Ki
        self.Kd = Kd
    def compute(self, setpoint, measured_value):
        error = setpoint - measured_value
        return self.Kp * error

def mutate(individual): individual[random.randint(0, len(individual) - 1)] = 1 - individual[random.randint(0, len(individual) - 1)]

def factorial(n):
    try:
        return n * factorial(n-1)
    except RecursionError:
        return 'Recursion limit exceeded.'

def divide(a, b):
    if b != 0:
        return a / b
    else:
        return 'Cannot divide by zero'

def fibonacci(n):
    a, b = 0, 1
    for _ in range(n):
        a, b = b, a + b
    return a

def list_dir(root):
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root):
        level = root.replace(root, '').count(os.sep)
        indent = ' ' * 4 * level
        print(f'{indent}{os.path.basename(root)}/')
        sub_indent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
        for f in files:
            print(f'{sub_indent}{f}')

def search_file(folder, target):
    for root, _, files in os.walk(folder):
        if target in files:
            return os.path.join(root, target)
    return 'File not found'

def automate_data_entry(data):
    wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
    sheet = wb.active
    sheet['A1'] = 'Name'
    sheet['B1'] = 'Age'
    row = 2
    for name, age in data.items():
        sheet[f'A{row}'] = name
        sheet[f'B{row}'] = age
        row += 1
    wb.save('data.xlsx')

def monitor_active_browser_tab():
    while True:
        active_window = gw.getActiveWindow()
        if any(browser in active_window.title for browser in ['Google Chrome', 'Mozilla Firefox', 'Opera GX', 'Microsoft Edge', 'Safari']):
            print(f'Current active browser tab: {active_window.title}')
        time.sleep(1)

def calculate_bmi(height, weight):
    bmi = weight / (height ** 2)
    return round(bmi, 2)

def caloric_intake(age, gender, activity):
    if gender == 'male':
        base = 10 * weight + 6.25 * height - 5 * age + 5
    else:
        base = 10 * weight + 6.25 * height - 5 * age - 161
    return base * activity

def on_press(key):
    global count
    count += 1
    if count > 100:
        print('Potential keylogging attempt detected.')

def check_input(user_input):
    if re.search(r'(DROP[\s]+TABLE)|(SELECT[\s]+\*)', user_input, re.IGNORECASE):
        print('Potential SQL injection attempt detected.')

def check_for_xss(user_input):
    if re.search(r'<[\s]*script[\s]*>', user_input, re.IGNORECASE):
        print('Potential XSS attack detected.')

def detect_exfiltration(packet):
    if len(packet) > 1000:
        print('Potential data exfiltration detected.')

    try:
        obj = pickle.loads(serialized_obj)
    except:
        print('Insecure deserialization attempt detected.')

def check_file_integrity(filename, original_hash):
    with open(filename, 'rb') as f:
        file_hash = hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()
    if file_hash != original_hash:
        print('File integrity compromised.')

def check_input_length(user_input):
    if len(user_input) > 1024:
        print('Potential buffer overflow attempt detected.')

def check_for_cryptojacking():
    cpu_usage = psutil.cpu_percent()
    if cpu_usage > 90:
        print('Potential cryptojacking detected.')

class Watcher:
    def run(self):
        event_handler = FileSystemEventHandler()
        observer = Observer()
        observer.schedule(event_handler, 'path/to/watch', recursive=False)
        observer.start()
        event_handler.on_modified = self.check_file_access
    def check_file_access(self, event):
        print(f'File accessed: {event.src_path}')

def arp_display(packet):
    if packet[ARP].op == 2:
        print(f'ARP Spoofing detected from: {packet[ARP].psrc}')

def detect_ddos(packet):
    ip = packet[1].src
    ip_counts[ip] = ip_counts.get(ip, 0) + 1
    if ip_counts[ip] > 100:
        print(f'DDoS attack detected from {ip}')

def custom_data_collator(batch):
    input_ids = [item[0] for item in batch]
    attn_masks = [item[1] for item in batch]
    return {'input_ids': torch.stack(input_ids), 'attention_mask': torch.stack(attn_masks)}

class OnTheFly(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, txt_list, tokenizer):
        self.txt_list = txt_list
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.txt_list)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        txt = self.txt_list[idx]
        encodings_dict = self.tokenizer(txt, truncation=True, max_length=512, padding='max_length')
        return torch.tensor(encodings_dict['input_ids']), torch.tensor(encodings_dict['attention_mask'])

def prepare_model_for_training(model_name):
    model = LlamaForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_name, quantization_config=bnb_config, device_map={'': 0})
    model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(model)
    peft_config = LoraConfig(lora_alpha=8, lora_dropout=0.05, r=4, task_type='CAUSAL_LM')
    return PeftModel(model, peft_config)

def train_model(model, train_dataset, val_dataset):
    training_args = TrainingArguments(output_dir='output_dir', per_device_train_batch_size=10)
    trainer = SFTTrainer(model=model, args=training_args, train_dataset=train_dataset, eval_dataset=val_dataset)
    trainer.train()
    return trainer

def save_trained_model(trainer, path):
    trainer.model.save_pretrained(path)
    torch.save(trainer.model.state_dict(), f'{path}/model_weights.pth')

    dataset1 = load_dataset('dataset1')

    return concatenate_datasets([dataset1, dataset2])

def setup_tensorboard(log_dir):
    return SummaryWriter(log_dir=log_dir)

def setup_optimizer_scheduler(model):
    optimizer = AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-4)
    scheduler = get_linear_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, num_warmup_steps=0, num_training_steps=10000)
    return optimizer, scheduler

def evaluate_model(trainer, val_dataset):
    metrics = trainer.evaluate(eval_dataset=val_dataset)
    return metrics

def setup_sft_training_args():
    return TrainingArguments(output_dir='output', per_device_train_batch_size=8, learning_rate=2e-4, num_train_epochs=3)

def initialize_sft_trainer(model, args, train_dataset, val_dataset):
    return SFTTrainer(model=model, args=args, train_dataset=train_dataset, eval_dataset=val_dataset)

def setup_sft_callbacks():
    return [TensorBoardCallback()]

def finalize_and_push_model(output_dir, hub_name):
    model = AutoPeftModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(output_dir, device_map='auto', torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)
    model = model.merge_and_unload()
    model.save_pretrained(output_dir)
    model.push_to_hub(hub_name)

def organize_files(folder_path):
    for filename in os.listdir(folder_path):
        file_ext = filename.split('.')[-1]
        if not os.path.exists(file_ext):
            os.makedirs(file_ext)
        shutil.move(os.path.join(folder_path, filename), os.path.join(folder_path, file_ext, filename))

def scan_ports(host, port_range):
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=50) as executor:
        for port in port_range:
            future = executor.submit(check_port, host, port)

def check_port(host, port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.settimeout(1)
    result = s.connect_ex((host, port))
    if result == 0:
        print(f'Port {port} is open')

def fft(x):
    N = len(x)
    if N <= 1: return x
    even, odd = fft(x[0::2]), fft(x[1::2])
    T = [np.exp(-2j * np.pi * k / N) * odd[k] for k in range(N // 2)]
    return [even[k] + T[k] for k in range(N // 2)] + [even[k] - T[k] for k in range(N // 2)]

class NeuralNetwork:
    def __init__(self, layers):
        self.layers, self.weights = layers, [np.random.randn(y, x) for x, y in zip(layers[:-1], layers[1:])]
    def feedforward(self, a):
        for w in self.weights:
            a = 1 / (1 + np.exp(-np.dot(w, a)))
        return a

def genetic_algorithm(target):
    population = [''.join(random.choice('01') for _ in range(len(target))) for _ in range(20)]
    while True:
        scores = [(x, sum(c1 != c2 for c1, c2 in zip(x, target))) for x in population]
        scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
        if scores[0][1] == 0: return scores[0][0]
        population = [mate(scores[i][0], scores[i+1][0]) for i in range(0, len(scores), 2)]

def markov_chain(text, n=2):
    chain, words = {}, text.split()
    for i in range(len(words) - n):
        key, next_word = tuple(words[i:i + n - 1]), words[i + n - 1]
        chain[key] = chain.get(key, []) + [next_word]
    return chain

def gaussian_elimination(A, b):
    n = len(b)
    for i in range(n):
        max_row = max(range(i, n), key=lambda x: abs(A[x][i]))
        A[[i, max_row]], b[i], b[max_row] = A[[max_row, i]], b[max_row], b[i]
        for j in range(i+1, n):
            factor = A[j][i] / A[i][i]
            A[j] -= factor * A[i]
            b[j] -= factor * b[i]
    return b

def euler_method(f, a, b, y0, h):
    n, y = int((b - a) / h), y0
    for i in range(n):
        y += h * f(a, y)
        a += h
    return y

def monte_carlo_pi(n):
    count = sum(1 for _ in range(n) if random.random()**2 + random.random()**2 <= 1)
    return 4 * count / n

def backpropagation(layers, weights, biases, activation, x, y):
    activations = [x]
    for w, b in zip(weights, biases):
        x = activation(np.dot(w, x) + b)
        activations.append(x)
    delta = (activations[-1] - y) * activation(activations[-1], True)
    for i in range(len(layers) - 2, 0, -1):
        delta = np.dot(weights[i].T, delta) * activation(activations[i], True)
        weights[i] -= delta * activations[i-1].T

def handle_value(value):
    if value == 'a':
        return 'Got a'
    elif value == 'b':
        return 'Got b'
    else:
        assert_never(value)

def FFT(x):
    N = x.shape[0]
    if N <= 1: return x
    even = FFT(x[::2])
    odd = FFT(x[1::2])
    T = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(N // 2) / N)
    return np.concatenate([even + T * odd, even - T * odd])

def newton_raphson(f, f_prime, x0, tol=1e-5):
    x = x0
    while abs(f(x)) > tol:
        x -= f(x) / f_prime(x)
    return x

def runge_kutta_4(f, x0, y0, h, n):
    x, y = x0, y0
    for i in range(n):
        k1 = h * f(x, y)
        k2 = h * f(x + 0.5 * h, y + 0.5 * k1)
        k3 = h * f(x + 0.5 * h, y + 0.5 * k2)
        k4 = h * f(x + h, y + k3)
        y += (k1 + 2 * k2 + 2 * k3 + k4) / 6
        x += h

def lagrange_interpolation(x_values, y_values, x):
    result = 0
    for i in range(len(y_values)):
        term = y_values[i]
        for j in range(len(x_values)):
            if j != i:
                term = term * (x - x_values[j]) / (x_values[i] - x_values[j])
        result += term
    return result

def jacobi(A, b, tol=1e-5):
    x = np.zeros_like(b)
    while True:
        x_new = np.dot(np.linalg.inv(np.diag(np.diag(A))), b - np.dot(A - np.diag(np.diag(A)), x))
        if np.allclose(x, x_new, tol):
            return x_new
        x = x_new

def gauss_seidel(A, b, tol=1e-5):
    x = np.zeros_like(b)
    while True:
        x_new = np.zeros_like(x)
        for i in range(A.shape[0]):
            s1 = np.dot(A[i, :i], x[:i])
            s2 = np.dot(A[i, i + 1:], x[i + 1:])
            x_new[i] = (b[i] - s1 - s2) / A[i, i]
        if np.allclose(x, x_new, atol=tol):
            return x_new
        x = x_new

def estimate_pi(n):
    points_inside_circle = 0
    for _ in range(n):
        x, y = random.random(), random.random()
        if x ** 2 + y ** 2 <= 1:
            points_inside_circle += 1
    return 4 * points_inside_circle / n

def bisection(f, a, b, tol=1e-5):
    c = (a + b) / 2.0
    while abs(f(c)) > tol:
        if f(c) * f(a) < 0:
            b = c
        else:
            a = c
        c = (a + b) / 2.0
    return c

def euler_method(f, y0, t0, t1, h):
    y, t = y0, t0
    while t <= t1:
        y += h * f(t, y)
        t += h
    return y

def dijkstra(graph, start):
    distances = {node: float('inf') for node in graph}
    distances[start] = 0
    pq = [(0, start)]
    while pq:
        current_distance, current_node = heapq.heappop(pq)
        if current_distance > distances[current_node]:
            continue
        for neighbor, weight in graph[current_node].items():
            distance = current_distance + weight
            if distance < distances[neighbor]:
                distances[neighbor] = distance
                heapq.heappush(pq, (distance, neighbor))
    return distances

def list_dict(list_one, list_two):
    """Create a dictionary of elements in the two lists which have the same index."""
    assert len(list_one) == len(list_two), "lists must be same length"
    dict = {}
    for i in range(len(list_one)):
        dict[list_one[i]] = list_two[i]
    return dict

def delete_desktop_file(file_name):
    desktop = os.path.join(os.path.join(os.environ['USERPROFILE']), 'Desktop')
    file_path = os.path.join(desktop, file_name)
    if os.path.exists(file_path):
        os.remove(file_path)
    else:
        print(f'File {file_path} not found.')

def open_desktop_file(file_name):
    desktop = os.path.join(os.path.join(os.environ['USERPROFILE']), 'Desktop')
    file_path = os.path.join(desktop, file_name)
    if os.path.exists(file_path):
        subprocess.run(['start', file_path], shell=True)
    else:
        print(f'File {file_path} not found.')

def launch_program():
    program_relative_path = 'Program Files (x86)\\Steam\\Steam.exe'
    for drive in string.ascii_uppercase:
        full_path = f'{drive}:\\{program_relative_path}'
        if os.path.exists(full_path):
            subprocess.Popen(full_path)
            return
    print('Steam.exe not found in expected directories.')

def check_fps():
    t1, cnt, intv = time.time(), 0, 1
    nxt = t1 + intv
    while True:
        cnt += 1
        time.sleep(0.001)
        t2 = time.time()
        if t2 >= nxt:
            print(f'FPS: {cnt / intv}')
            cnt, t1 = 0, t2
            nxt = t2 + intv

class Singleton:
    _instance = None
    def __new__(cls):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = super(Singleton, cls).__new__(cls)
        return cls._instance

def timer(func):
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        start = time.time()
        result = func(*args, **kwargs)
        end = time.time()
        print(f'Time elapsed: {end - start}')
        return result
    return wrapper

def log_function_call(func):
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        logging.info(f'Function {func.__name__} called.')
        return func(*args, **kwargs)
    return wrapper

    start_urls = ['http://example.com']

        self.log(f'Visited {response.url}')

class Blockchain:
    def __init__(self):
        self.chain = []
        self.current_transactions = []
    def new_block(self, proof):
        block = {
            'index': len(self.chain) + 1,
            'transactions': self.current_transactions,
            'proof': proof
        }
        self.current_transactions = []
        self.chain.append(block)
        return block

        self.index = index
        self.data = data
        self.previous_hash = previous_hash

    def calculate_hash(self):
        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(self.__dict__, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()

def repeat(n):
    def decorator(func):
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            for _ in range(n):
                func(*args, **kwargs)
        return wrapper
    return decorator

def factorial(n):
    if n == 0:
        return 1
    else:
        return n * factorial(n-1)

def reactions(y, t):
    A, B = y
    k1, k2 = 0.1, 0.2
    dA_dt = -k1*A + k2*B
    dB_dt = k1*A - k2*B
    return [dA_dt, dB_dt]

def geothermal_energy(temperature, volume):
    density = 2500
    specific_heat = 1000
    energy =  density * specific_heat * volume * temperature
    return energy

class ChatConsumer(AsyncWebsocketConsumer):
    async def connect(self):
        await self.accept()
    async def disconnect(self, close_code):
        pass
    async def receive(self, text_data):
        text_data_json = json.loads(text_data)
        message = text_data_json['message']
        await self.send(text_data=json.dumps({'message': message}))

def solve(bo):
    find = find_empty(bo)
    if not find:
        return True
    else:
        row, col = find
    for i in range(1,10):
        if valid(bo, i, (row, col)):
            bo[row][col] = i
            if solve(bo):
                return True
            bo[row][col] = 0
    return False

class Net(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super(Net, self).__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 6, 3)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(6, 16, 3)
    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.max_pool2d(F.relu(self.conv1(x)), (2, 2))
        x = F.max_pool2d(F.relu(self.conv2(x)), 2)
        return x

def update_chat(self, generated_text):
    entire_chat_lines = self.chatbox.toPlainText().split('\n')
    last_you_line_index = None
    for i, line in reversed(list(enumerate(entire_chat_lines))):
        if 'You: ' in line:
            last_you_line_index = i
            break
    self.chatbox.append(f'Bot: {generated_text}')

def sum_of_squares(n):
    return sum([i*i for i in range(n+1)])

def find_duplicates(lst):
    seen = set()
    duplicates = set()
    for x in lst:
        if x in seen:
            duplicates.add(x)
        else:
            seen.add(x)
    return duplicates

def upload_files(filenames, upload_url='https://example.com/upload'):
    for file in filenames:
        with open(file, 'rb') as f:
            content = f.read()
        response = requests.post(upload_url, files={'file': content})
        if response.status_code == 200:
            print(f'Successfully uploaded {file}')
        else:
            print(f'Failed to upload {file}')

def process_data(data):
    processed = []
    for d in data:
        processed_item = {'id': d.get('id', None), 'value': d.get('value', 0) * 2}
        processed.append(processed_item)
    return processed

def validate_form(form):
    if 'username' in form and 'password' in form:
        username = form['username']
        password = form['password']
        if re.match(r'^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,}$', username) and re.match(r'^[a-zA-Z0-9@#$%^&+=]{8,}$', password):
            return True
    return False

def send_email(recipient, subject, message):
    server = smtplib.SMTP('smtp.gmail.com', 587)
    server.starttls()
    server.login('youremail@gmail.com', 'yourpassword')
    msg = f'Subject: {subject}

{message}'
    server.sendmail('youremail@gmail.com', recipient, msg)
    server.quit()

def database_operations(db_config):
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(**db_config)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        # Create a table
        cursor.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, username VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, email VARCHAR(255))')

        # Insert data into the table
        cursor.execute("INSERT INTO users (username, email) VALUES ('john_doe', 'john@example.com')")
        cursor.execute("INSERT INTO users (username, email) VALUES ('jane_doe', 'jane@example.com')")

        # Commit changes
        conn.commit()

        # Retrieve data
        cursor.execute('SELECT * FROM users')
        records = cursor.fetchall()
        for record in records:
            print(record)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f'Error: {e}')
    finally:
        if conn:
            cursor.close()
            conn.close()

def image_processing(image_path):
    try:
        # Load the image
        img = Image.open(image_path)

        # Resize the image
        img = img.resize((300, 300))

        # Rotate the image
        img = img.rotate(90)

        # Convert to grayscale
        img = ImageOps.grayscale(img)

        # Save the processed image
        img.save('processed_image.jpg')
    except Exception as e:
        print(f'Error: {e}')

def web_scraping(url):
    try:
        # Fetch HTML content
        response = requests.get(url)
        if response.status_code != 200:
            return f'Failed to fetch data from {url}'

        # Parse HTML
        soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, 'html.parser')

        # Extract and print all paragraph texts
        for p in soup.find_all('p'):
            print(p.get_text())
    except Exception as e:
        print(f'Error: {e}')

def machine_learning_model(data, labels):
    # Split data into training and test sets
    X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(data, labels, test_size=0.2)

    # Create and train the model
    model = RandomForestClassifier()
    model.fit(X_train, y_train)

    # Make predictions
    y_pred = model.predict(X_test)

    # Calculate accuracy
    acc = accuracy_score(y_test, y_pred)
    return acc

class Block:
    def __init__(self, index, transactions, previous_hash):
        self.index = index
        self.transactions = transactions
        self.previous_hash = previous_hash
        self.hash = self.calculate_hash()

    def calculate_hash(self):
        block_string = json.dumps(self.__dict__, sort_keys=True).encode()
        return hashlib.sha256(block_string).hexdigest()

def blockchain_operations():
    # Create genesis block
    genesis_block = Block(0, [], '0')

    # Create new block with transactions
    new_block = Block(1, ['tx1', 'tx2'], genesis_block.hash)

    # Validate the chain
    if new_block.previous_hash == genesis_block.hash:
        print('Blockchain is valid.')
    else:
        print('Blockchain is invalid.')

def nlp_operations(text):
    # Tokenize the text
    tokens = word_tokenize(text)

    # Apply stemming
    stemmer = PorterStemmer()
    stemmed_tokens = [stemmer.stem(token) for token in tokens]

    # Perform Named Entity Recognition
    named_entities = ne_chunk(pos_tag(tokens))

    return {'tokens': tokens, 'stemmed': stemmed_tokens, 'named_entities': named_entities}

def get_max(numbers):
    if not numbers:
        return 'List is empty'
    max_val = numbers[0]
    for num in numbers:
        if num > max_val:
            max_val = num
    return max_val

def set_operations(s):
    s.add(1)
    s.add(2)
    s.discard(3)

def fibonacci(n):
    sequence = []
    a, b = 0, 1
    for i in range(n):
        sequence.append(a)
        a, b = b, a + b
    return sequence

def sql_query(query, db_config):
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_config)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute(query)
        conn.commit()
    except sqlite3.Error as e:
        print(f'SQLite error: {e}')
    finally:
        if cursor:
            cursor.close()
        if conn:
            conn.close()

def web_scraper(url):
    try:
        page = requests.get(url)
        page.raise_for_status()
        soup = BeautifulSoup(page.content, 'html.parser')
        title = soup.find('title')
        if title:
            return title.text
        else:
            return 'Title not found'
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'HTTP Error: {e}'

def backup_files(src_folder, dest_folder):
    if os.path.exists(dest_folder):
        shutil.rmtree(dest_folder)
    try:
        shutil.copytree(src_folder, dest_folder)
    except shutil.Error as e:
        print(f'Backup error: {e}')

        urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, path)
    except urllib.error.URLError as e:

    except Exception as e:
        print(f'An unexpected error occurred: {e}')

def text_analyzer(text):

        return 'Text is empty or null'
    words = text.split()
    word_count = len(words)
    return word_count

def list_duplicates(seq):
    tally = Counter(seq)
    return [item for item, count in tally.items() if count > 1]

def factorial(n):
    if n < 0:
        return 'Negative numbers do not have factorials'
    elif n == 0:
        return 1
    else:
        return n * factorial(n-1)

def calculate_interest(principal, rate, time):
    if not isinstance(principal, (int, float)) or not isinstance(rate, (int, float)) or not isinstance(time, (int, float)):
        return 'Invalid input types'
    return principal * (1 + rate / 100) ** time

def square_root(number):
    if number < 0:
        return cmath.sqrt(number)
    return number ** 0.5

def sort_list(unsorted_list):
    if unsorted_list is None:
        return 'List is null'
    elif len(unsorted_list) == 0:
        return 'List is empty'
    return sorted(unsorted_list)

def fibonacci(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return 'Invalid input'
    fib_list = [0, 1]
    [fib_list.append(fib_list[-1] + fib_list[-2]) for _ in range(2, n)]
    return fib_list[:n]

def binary_search(arr, target):
    if not arr:
        return 'Array is empty'
    low, high = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        low, high = (mid + 1, high) if arr[mid] < target else (low, mid - 1)

def prime_factors(n):
    factors = []
    for divisor in [2, 3]:
        while n % divisor == 0:
            factors.append(divisor)
            n //= divisor
    for divisor in range(5, int(n ** 0.5) + 1, 6):
        while n % divisor == 0:
            factors.append(divisor)
            n //= divisor
        while n % (divisor + 2) == 0:
            factors.append(divisor + 2)
            n //= (divisor + 2)
    if n > 1:
        factors.append(n)
    return factors

def quicksort(arr):
    if len(arr) <= 1:
        return arr
    pivot = arr[0]
    left = [x for x in arr[1:] if x < pivot]
    middle = [x for x in arr if x == pivot]
    right = [x for x in arr[1:] if x > pivot]
    return quicksort(left) + middle + quicksort(right)

def factorial(n, acc=1):
    if n == 0:
        return acc
    return factorial(n-1, n*acc)

def square_root(x):
    if x < 0:
        return 'Invalid input'
    return sqrt(x)

def bubble_sort(arr):
    n = len(arr)
    for i in range(n):
        swapped = False
        for j in range(0, n-i-1):
            if arr[j] > arr[j+1]:
                arr[j], arr[j+1] = arr[j+1], arr[j]
                swapped = True
        if not swapped:
            break

def safe_strptime(date_str, format):
    try:
        return datetime.strptime(date_str, format)
    except ValueError:
        return 'Invalid date format'

def process_data(data):
    if not data or not all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) for x in data):
        return 'Invalid input'
    processed = [x**2 for x in data]
    return sum(processed) / len(processed)

def main():
    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
    try:
        loop.run_until_complete(async_task())
    finally:
        loop.close()

async def async_task():
    await asyncio.sleep(1)
    print('Done')

def download_files(file_urls):
    for url in file_urls:
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['wget', url], check=True)
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
            print(f'Error during downloading {url}: {e}')

def calculate_price(items):
    try:
        return sum([item['price'] for item in items if 'price' in item and isinstance(item['price'], (int, float))])
    except TypeError:
        return 'Invalid input'

def parse_json(json_str):
    try:
        return json.loads(json_str)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return f'Invalid JSON: {e}'

def fetch_data_from_api(api_url):
    try:
        response = requests.get(api_url, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def csv_reader(file_path, encoding='utf-8'):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding=encoding) as f:
            reader = csv.reader(f)
            for row in reader:
                print(row)
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        print(f'File not found: {e}')
    except csv.Error as e:
        print(f'CSV Error: {e}')

def web_crawler(start_url, rate_limit=1):
    visited = set()
    stack = [start_url]
    while stack:
        sleep(rate_limit)
        url = stack.pop()
        url_hash = md5(url.encode()).hexdigest()
        if url_hash not in visited:
            visited.add(url_hash)
            links = scrape_links(url)
            stack.extend(links)

def web_scraper(urls):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    for url in urls:
        try:
            r = requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=10)
            r.raise_for_status()
            soup = BeautifulSoup(r.content, 'html.parser')
            title = soup.title.string if soup.title else 'No title'
        except (requests.HTTPError, requests.Timeout) as e:
            print(f'Error: {e}')

def matrix_operations(A, B):
    if A.shape[1] != B.shape[0]:
        return 'Incompatible dimensions'
    dot_product = np.dot(A, B)
    element_wise_sum = np.add(A, B)
    return {'dot_product': dot_product, 'element_wise_sum': element_wise_sum}

def neural_network(X, y):
    early_stopping = EarlyStopping(monitor='val_loss', patience=3)
    model = keras.Sequential([
        keras.layers.Dense(128, activation='relu'),
        keras.layers.Dropout(0.5),
        keras.layers.Dense(10)
    ])
    model.compile(optimizer='adam', loss='sparse_categorical_crossentropy', metrics=['accuracy'])
    model.fit(X, y, epochs=20, callbacks=[early_stopping], validation_split=0.2)

def scrape_webpage(url):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    try:
        page = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
        page.raise_for_status()
        soup = BeautifulSoup(page.text, 'html.parser')
        titles = soup.find_all('h1')
        return titles
    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
        print(f'HTTP Error: {e}')
        return None

def data_augmentation(images):
    augmented_images = []
    for img in images:
        angle = random.uniform(-30, 30)
        tform = AffineTransform(translation=(0, 0))
        rotated_img = warp(img, tform)
        augmented_images.append(rotated_img)
    return augmented_images

def parse_xml(xml_string):
    root = ET.fromstring(xml_string)
    namespace = {'ns': 'http://www.example.com'}
    for child in root.findall('.//ns:element', namespace):
        if 'attribute' in child.attrib:
            print(child.tag, child.attrib)

def generate_report(data):
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
    with open('report.txt', 'w') as f:
        f.write(f'Report generated on: {timestamp}\n')
        for item in data:
            f.write(f'{item}\n')

def process_video(video_path):
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
    try:
        while True:
            ret, frame = cap.read()
            if not ret:
                break
            cv2.imshow('Frame', frame)
            if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord('q'):
                break
    finally:
        cap.release()
        cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def fetch_multiple_apis(api_urls):
    responses = {}
    with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
        future_to_url = {executor.submit(requests.get, url): url for url in api_urls}
        for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed(future_to_url):
            url = future_to_url[future]
            try:
                data = future.result().json()
                responses[url] = data
            except Exception as e:
                responses[url] = str(e)
    return responses

def load_and_save_images(image_paths):
    image_objects = [(Path(path), Image.open(path)) for path in image_paths]
    for path_obj, img_obj in image_objects:
        new_path = path_obj.parent / f'new_{path_obj.name}'
        img_obj.save(new_path)

def analyze_audio(audio_path):
    audio_file = AudioSegment.from_file(audio_path)
    loudness = audio_file.dBFS
    samples = np.array(audio_file.get_array_of_samples())
    sampling_rate = audio_file.frame_rate
    fft_output = np.fft.fft(samples)
    return {'loudness': loudness, 'fft_output': fft_output, 'sampling_rate': sampling_rate}

def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path):
    full_text = ''
    with open(pdf_path, 'rb') as f:
        pdf_reader = PyPDF2.PdfFileReader(f)
        for i in range(pdf_reader.numPages):
            page = pdf_reader.getPage(i)
            full_text += page.extract_text() + '\n'
    return full_text

def scrape_webpage(url):
    session = requests.Session()
    retry = Retry(total=5, backoff_factor=0.1, status_forcelist=[ 500, 502, 503, 504 ])
    adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retry)
    session.mount('http://', adapter)
    session.mount('https://', adapter)
    page = session.get(url, timeout=5)
    soup = BeautifulSoup(page.content, 'html.parser')
    return soup.find_all('a')

def process_csv(file_path):
    df = pd.read_csv(file_path, infer_datetime_format=True, low_memory=False)
    df.dropna(inplace=True)
    for col in df.columns:
        if df[col].dtype == object:
            df[col] = df[col].str.strip()
    return df

def calculate_statistics(data):
    data_np = np.array(data)
    mean = np.mean(data_np)
    variance = np.var(data_np)
    return {'mean': mean, 'variance': variance}

def read_and_resize_images(image_paths):
    resized_images = []
    for path in image_paths:
        with Image.open(path) as img:
            img_resized = img.resize((100, 100), Image.ANTIALIAS)
            resized_images.append(img_resized)
    return resized_images

def query_database(query, params):
    conn = sqlite3.connect('database.db')
    try:
        with conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(query, params)
            return cursor.fetchall()
    except sqlite3.Error as e:
        print(f'An error occurred: {e}')
        return None

def tensorflow_model(train_data, train_labels):
    model = tf.keras.Sequential([
        tf.keras.layers.Dense(64, activation='relu'),
        tf.keras.layers.Dense(1)
    ])
    model.compile(optimizer='adam', loss='mse', metrics=['mae'])
    early_stopping = EarlyStopping(monitor='val_loss', patience=3)
    model.fit(train_data, train_labels, epochs=50, validation_split=0.2, callbacks=[early_stopping])
    loss, mae = model.evaluate(train_data, train_labels)
    print(f'Model evaluation - Loss: {loss}, MAE: {mae}')

def process_text(text):
    text = text.lower()
    text = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text)
    text = re.sub(r'[^\w\s]', '', text)
    return text.replace(' ', '_')

def fetch_and_parse(url):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    if 'captcha' in response.text.lower():
        client = AnticaptchaClient('api_key')
        task = ImageToTextTask(response.content)
        job = client.createTask(task)
        job.join()
        captcha_solution = job.get_captcha_text()
        response = requests.get(url, params={'captcha_solution': captcha_solution}, headers=headers)
    return BeautifulSoup(response.text, 'html.parser').find_all('a')

def rate_limited_request(url):
    time.sleep(1)  # Rate limiting
    return requests.get(url)

def api_interaction(api_url):
    response = rate_limited_request(api_url)
    if response.status_code == 200:
        return response.json()
    else:
        return {'error': 'Failed to fetch data'}

def batch_processing(data):
    if not data:
        return 'No data provided'
    threads = []
    for i in range(0, len(data), 10):
        batch = data[i:i+10]
        thread = Thread(target=process_batch, args=(batch,))
        thread.start()
        threads.append(thread)
    for t in threads:
        t.join()

def calculate_metrics(y_true, y_pred):
    if len(y_true) != len(y_pred) or len(y_true) == 0:
        return 'Invalid input sizes'
    accuracy = accuracy_score(y_true, y_pred)
    precision = precision_score(y_true, y_pred, average='weighted')
    recall = recall_score(y_true, y_pred, average='weighted')
    f1 = f1_score(y_true, y_pred, average='weighted')
    return {'accuracy': accuracy, 'precision': precision, 'recall': recall, 'f1': f1}

def image_processing(image_path):
    if not image_path:
        return 'Invalid image path'
    image = cv2.imread(image_path, cv2.IMREAD_UNCHANGED)
    if image is None:
        return 'Could not read the image'
    gray_image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    normalized_image = cv2.normalize(gray_image, None, alpha=0, beta=1, norm_type=cv2.NORM_MINMAX, dtype=cv2.CV_32F)
    return normalized_image

def data_storage(data, db_path):
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute('BEGIN TRANSACTION')
        cursor.execute('INSERT INTO table_name (column1, column2) VALUES (?, ?)', (data['key1'], data['key2']))
        conn.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        conn.rollback()
        return f'Error: {e}'
    finally:
        conn.close()

def csv_processing(csv_file):
    try:
        df = pd.read_csv(csv_file)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return 'File not found'
    except pd.errors.EmptyDataError:
        return 'File is empty'
    except pd.errors.ParserError:
        return 'Error during parsing'
    return df

def web_scraping(url):
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
    except RequestException as e:
        return f'Failed to fetch URL: {e}'
    html_content = response.text
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html_content, 'html.parser')
    titles = soup.find_all('h1', {'class': 'specific-class'})
    for title in titles:
        print(title.text.strip())
    return titles

def api_request(api_url):
    page = 1
    while True:
        paginated_url = f'{api_url}?page={page}'
        try:
            response = requests.get(paginated_url, timeout=10)
            response.raise_for_status()
        except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
            return f'Error: {e}'
        data = response.json()
        if not data:
            break
        for item in data:
            print(f"Name: {item.get('name', 'N/A')}, Age: {item.get('age', 'N/A')}")
        page += 1

def data_transformation(data):
    if not all(isinstance(item, list) and len(item) == 3 for item in data):
        return 'Invalid data format'
    transformed_data = [{'name': item[0], 'age': item[1], 'country': item[2]} for item in data]
    return transformed_data

def file_read(file_path, encoding='utf-8'):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding=encoding) as f:
            content = f.read()
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return 'File not found'
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return 'Encoding error'
    return content

def list_duplicates(seq):
    tally = defaultdict(list)
    for i, item in enumerate(seq):
        tally[item].append(i)
    return [(key, locs) for key, locs in tally.items() if len(locs) > 1]

def web_crawler(start_url, max_depth=3, rate_limit=2):
    def crawl(url, depth):
        if depth > max_depth:
            return
        try:
            time.sleep(rate_limit)
            response = requests.get(url)
            response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.RequestException as e:
            print(f'Failed to fetch {url}: {e}')
            return
        soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, 'lxml')
        links = [a['href'] for a in soup.find_all('a', href=True)]
        print(f'Crawled {url} at depth {depth}')
        for link in links:
            crawl(link, depth+1)
    crawl(start_url, 0)

def resize_images(folder_path, output_size=(200, 200)):
    for filename in os.listdir(folder_path):
        if not filename.lower().endswith(('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg')):
            continue
        try:
            img = Image.open(os.path.join(folder_path, filename))
            img = img.resize(output_size, Image.ANTIALIAS)
            img.save(os.path.join(folder_path, f'resized_{filename}'))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f'Failed to resize {filename}: {e}')

def parse_logs(log_file, level='ERROR'):
    error_logs = []
    with open(log_file, 'r') as f:
        for line in f:
            if level in line:
                error_logs.append(line.strip())
    return error_logs

        print(f'Processing: {batch}')
    for i in range(0, len(items), batch_size):
        batch = items[i:i+batch_size]

            process_batch(batch)

def web_scraper(url, timeout=5):
    try:
        page = requests.get(url, timeout=timeout)
        page.raise_for_status()
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Failed to fetch {url}: {e}'
    soup = BeautifulSoup(page.content, 'lxml')
    titles = soup.select('div.title > h1, div.title > h2')
    for title in titles:
        print(title.get_text())

def machine_learning_model(X, y):
    scaler = StandardScaler()
    X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X)
    model = LogisticRegression()
    params = {'C': [0.01, 0.1, 1, 10], 'penalty': ['l1', 'l2']}
    grid = GridSearchCV(model, params, cv=5)
    grid.fit(X_scaled, y)
    return grid.best_estimator_

def text_analyzer(text):
    text = text.lower()
    text = text.translate(str.maketrans('', '', string.punctuation))
    words = text.split()
    word_count = collections.Counter(words)
    return word_count

def file_encryptor(file_path):
    key = Fernet.generate_key()
    cipher_suite = Fernet(key)
    with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
        content = f.read()
    encrypted_content = cipher_suite.encrypt(content)
    with open(f'{file_path}.enc', 'wb') as f:
        f.write(encrypted_content)
    return key

def web_crawler(start_url):
    visited = set()
    to_visit = deque([start_url])
    while to_visit:
        url = to_visit.popleft()
        if url in visited:
            continue
        try:
            response = requests.get(url)
            response.raise_for_status()
            html_content = response.text
        except requests.RequestException as e:
            print(f'Failed to fetch {url}: {e}')
            continue
        visited.add(url)
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html_content, 'lxml')
        for link in soup.find_all('a'):
            to_visit.append(link.get('href'))
