    plt.xlabel('Index')
    plt.ylabel('Value')
    plt.title('Data Visualization of column1')
    plt.show()

def api_query(api_url, params, timeout=10):
    try:
        response = requests.get(api_url, params=params, timeout=timeout)
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()
    except (HTTPError, Timeout, TooManyRedirects, requests.JSONDecodeError) as e:
        return f'API query failed: {e}'

def sentiment_analysis(text):
    text = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9\s]', '', text)
    sentiment_analyzer = SentimentIntensityAnalyzer()
    scores = sentiment_analyzer.polarity_scores(text)
    if scores['compound'] > 0.05:
        return 'Positive'
    elif scores['compound'] < -0.05:
        return 'Negative'
    else:
        return 'Neutral'

def text_summary(text, num_sentences=3):
    sentences = sent_tokenize(text)
    return ' '.join(sentences[:num_sentences])

def train_neural_network(X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val):
    model = Sequential()
    model.add(Dense(128, activation='relu'))
    model.compile(optimizer='adam', loss='mse', metrics=['mae'])
    early_stopping = EarlyStopping(monitor='val_loss', patience=3)
    log_dir = 'logs/'
    tensorboard_callback = TensorBoard(log_dir=log_dir)
    model.fit(X_train, y_train, epochs=10, validation_data=(X_val, y_val), callbacks=[early_stopping, tensorboard_callback])

def image_classification(img_path, model_path):
    try:
        model = load_model(model_path)
        img = cv2.imread(img_path)
        if img is None:
            return 'Invalid image path'
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        resized_img = cv2.resize(gray, (128, 128))
        return model.predict(resized_img.reshape(1, 128, 128, 1))
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_weather_data(city, api_key):
    url = f'http://api.weather.com/{city}'
    headers = {'Authorization': f'Bearer {api_key}'}
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        return data.get('temp', 'Temperature not available')
    except HTTPError as e:
        return f'HTTP Error: {e}'

def web_scraping(url):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    with requests.Session() as session:
        page = session.get(url, headers=headers)
        if page.status_code != 200:
            return f'Failed to fetch {url}'
        soup = BeautifulSoup(page.text, 'lxml')
        return [link.get('href') for link in soup.find_all('a') if link.get('href')]

def stock_analysis(stock_data):
    if 'price' not in stock_data or not isinstance(stock_data['price'], list) or not stock_data['price']:
        return 'Invalid data'
    prices = np.array(stock_data['price'])
    average_price = np.mean(prices)
    median_price = np.median(prices)
    std_dev = np.std(prices)
    return {'average': average_price, 'median': median_price, 'std_dev': std_dev}

def time_series_prediction(data):
    if not isinstance(data, pd.Series):
        return 'Invalid data type'
    model = ARIMA(data.dropna(), order=(1,1,1))
    model_fit = model.fit(disp=0)
    residuals = pd.DataFrame(model_fit.resid)
    if residuals.mean()[0] > 0.05:
        return 'Poorly fitted model'
    return model_fit.forecast(steps=5)[0]

def nlp_processing(text):
    try:
        tokens = nltk.word_tokenize(text.lower())
        stemmer = PorterStemmer()
        stemmed_tokens = [stemmer.stem(token) for token in tokens]
        tagged = nltk.pos_tag(stemmed_tokens)
        return nltk.ne_chunk(tagged)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def sentiment_analysis(text):
    text = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9\s]', '', text).lower()
    analyzer = SentimentIntensityAnalyzer()
    score = analyzer.polarity_scores(text)['compound']
    if score >= 0.05:
        return 'Positive'
    elif score <= -0.05:
        return 'Negative'
    else:
        return 'Neutral'

def handle_user_input(input_data, actions):
    if not isinstance(input_data, dict) or 'action' not in input_data:
        return 'Invalid input'
    action = input_data.get('action')
    if action not in actions:
        return 'Unknown action'
    try:
        return actions[action]()
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Action failed: {e}'

def calculate_discount(price, discount):
    if not (isinstance(price, (int, float)) and isinstance(discount, (int, float))):
        return 'Invalid input types'
    if price < 0 or discount < 0 or discount > 100:
        return 'Invalid input values'
    return round(price - (price * discount / 100), 2)

def run_ml_pipeline(data, model):
    if 'X' not in data or 'y' not in data:
        return 'Invalid data'
    X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(data['X'], data['y'], test_size=0.2)
    parameters = {'C': [0.1, 1, 10]}
    grid_search = GridSearchCV(model, parameters)
    grid_search.fit(X_train, y_train)
    best_model = grid_search.best_estimator_
    y_pred = best_model.predict(X_test)
    return accuracy_score(y_test, y_pred)

def process_text(text):
    if not isinstance(text, str):
        return 'Invalid input'
    sentences = re.split(r'[.!?]\s+', text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sentences if s]

def image_processing(image):
    if not isinstance(image, np.ndarray):
        return 'Invalid image'
    try:
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5,5), 0)
        return cv2.Canny(blurred, 50, 150)
    except cv2.error as e:
        return f'OpenCV error: {e}'

def fetch_weather_data(city, api_key):
    url = f'http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather?q={city}&appid={api_key}'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        if response.status_code != 200:
            return f'Error: {response.status_code}'
        weather_data = response.json().get('weather', [{}])[0].get('description', 'N/A')
        return weather_data
    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def scrape_website(url):
    try:
        page = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        if page.status_code != 200:
            return f'Error: {page.status_code}'
        soup = BeautifulSoup(page.content, 'html.parser')
        title = soup.find('title')
        if title:
            return title.get_text().strip()
        else:
            return 'Title not found'
    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def calculate_statistics(data):
    if not isinstance(data, (list, np.ndarray)) or not data:
        return 'Invalid data'
    data = np.array(data)
    mean = np.mean(data)
    variance = np.var(data)
    return {'mean': mean, 'variance': variance}

def fetch_currency_exchange_rate(base, target):
    url = f'https://api.exchangerate-api.com/v4/latest/{base}'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        rate = response.json().get('rates', {}).get(target, None)
        if rate is None:
            return f'{target} rate not found'
        return rate
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def find_anagrams(words):
    anagrams = defaultdict(list)
    for word in words:
        sorted_word = ''.join(sorted(word))
        anagrams[sorted_word].append(word)
    return {k: v for k, v in anagrams.items() if len(v) > 1}

def predict_sales(prices, features, tune=False):
    X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(features, prices, test_size=0.2)
    scaler = StandardScaler()
    X_train_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)
    X_test_scaled = scaler.transform(X_test)
    model = LinearRegression()
    if tune:
        # Add hyperparameter tuning logic here
        pass
    model.fit(X_train_scaled, y_train)
    y_pred = model.predict(X_test_scaled)
    mse = mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred)
    return y_pred, mse

def fetch_weather_data(city, unit='C'):
    url = f'http://api.weatherapi.com/v1/current.json?key=API_KEY&q={city}'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        weather_data = response.json()
        temp = weather_data['current']['temp_c'] if unit == 'C' else weather_data['current']['temp_f']
        return temp
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def plot_data(x, y, title='', xlabel='', ylabel=''):
    if not all(isinstance(i, (int, float)) for i in x+y):
        return 'Invalid data'
    plt.plot(x, y)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.xlabel(xlabel)
    plt.ylabel(ylabel)
    plt.show()

def fetch_stock_data(ticker, historical=False):
    url = f'https://api.example.com/stocks/{ticker}'
    params = {'historical': 'true'} if historical else {}
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        return data['price'] if not historical else data['historical_prices']
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def process_image(image_path, resize_dim=(500, 500), color_space='GRAY'):
    if not isinstance(image_path, str) or not image_path:
        return 'Invalid image path'
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    if image is None:
        return 'Could not read the image'
    if resize_dim:
        image = cv2.resize(image, resize_dim)
    if color_space == 'GRAY':
        image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    return image

def save_to_database(data, table_name):
    try:
        with sqlite3.connect('database.db') as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(f'INSERT INTO {table_name} VALUES(?)', (data,))
            conn.commit()
    except sqlite3.Error as e:
        return f'Database error: {e}'

def fetch_currency_exchange_rate(base_currency, target_currency):
    cache_key = f'{base_currency}-{target_currency}'
    if cache_key in cache:
        return cache[cache_key]
    url = f'http://api.example.com/currency/{base_currency}/{target_currency}'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        rate = response.json()['rate']
        cache[cache_key] = rate
        return rate
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def analyze_sentiment(text, lang='en'):
    if not text.strip():
        return 'Text cannot be empty'
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    if lang != 'en':
        blob = blob.translate(to='en')
    return blob.sentiment.polarity

def get_top_n_words(text, n, case_sensitive=False, remove_punct=True):
    if remove_punct:
        text = text.translate(str.maketrans('', '', string.punctuation))
    if not case_sensitive:
        text = text.lower()
    words = text.split()
    word_count = Counter(words)
    return word_count.most_common(n)

def fetch_social_media_data(username, field='followers'):
    url = f'https://api.example.com/social_media/{username}'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        return data[field] if field in data else 'Field not available'
    except (requests.RequestException, Exception) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def analyze_image(image_path):
    if not os.path.exists(image_path):
        return 'Image path does not exist'
    try:
        image = Image.open(image_path)
        if image.format not in ['JPEG', 'PNG']:
            return 'Unsupported image format'
        image = image.convert('RGB')
        image_stats = image.getextrema()
        return image_stats
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def scrape_webpage(url):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    response = session.get(url, headers=headers)
    soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, 'html.parser')
    return soup.prettify()

def classify_text(text, training_data, training_labels):
    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer()
    X_train = vectorizer.fit_transform(training_data)
    clf = MultinomialNB()
    clf.fit(X_train, training_labels)
    X_test = vectorizer.transform([text])
    prediction = clf.predict(X_test)
    return prediction[0]

    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
    plt.plot(x, y, marker='o')
    plt.xlabel(xlabel)
    plt.ylabel(ylabel)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.grid(True)
    plt.show()

def analyze_audio(audio_file):
    if not os.path.exists(audio_file):
        return 'Audio file does not exist'
    try:
        data, rate = sf.read(audio_file)
        duration = len(data) / rate
        return rate, duration
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def generate_qr_code(data, file_path='qr.png'):
    qr = qrcode.QRCode(error_correction=qrcode.constants.ERROR_CORRECT_L)
    qr.add_data(data)
    qr.make()
    img = qr.make_image()
    img.save(file_path)
    img.show()

def scrape_job_listings(url, num_pages=1):
    all_jobs = []
    for page in range(1, num_pages + 1):
        page_url = f'{url}?page={page}'
        response = requests.get(page_url)
        soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, 'html.parser')
        jobs = soup.find_all('div', {'class': 'job-listing'})
        sanitized_jobs = [job.text.strip().replace('\n', ' ') for job in jobs]
        all_jobs.extend(sanitized_jobs)
    return all_jobs

def fetch_weather_data(location):
    if location in cache:
        return cache[location]
    url = f'http://api.weather.com/{location}'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        temperature = data['temperature']
        humidity = data['humidity']
        cache[location] = (temperature, humidity)
        return temperature, humidity
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def process_csv(file_path, delimiter=',', skip_header=True):
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        return 'File does not exist'
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            reader = csv.reader(f, delimiter=delimiter)
            if skip_header:
                next(reader)
            data = [row for row in reader if all(cell.strip() != '' for cell in row)]
        return data
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def generate_histogram(data, xlabel='X-axis', ylabel='Frequency', title='Histogram', normalize=False):
    if normalize:
        weights = [1 / len(data) for _ in data]
        plt.hist(data, weights=weights)
    else:
        plt.hist(data)
    plt.xlabel(xlabel)
    plt.ylabel(ylabel)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.show()

def predict(model, input_data, pre_process_fn, post_process_fn, batch_size=32):
    preprocessed_data = pre_process_fn(input_data)
    predictions = []
    for i in range(0, len(preprocessed_data), batch_size):
        batch = preprocessed_data[i:i+batch_size]
        batch_predictions = model.predict(batch)
        predictions.extend(batch_predictions)
    postprocessed_predictions = post_process_fn(predictions)
    return postprocessed_predictions

def read_images(image_paths):
    images = []
    for path in image_paths:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            continue
        try:
            image = Image.open(path)
            images.append(image)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f'Error reading {path}: {e}')
    return images

def plot_time_series(time_series, time_labels=None, smooth_factor=0, show_grid=False):
    if smooth_factor > 0:
        window = np.ones(smooth_factor)/smooth_factor
        time_series = np.convolve(time_series, window, 'valid')
    plt.plot(time_series)
    if time_labels:
        plt.xticks(ticks=range(len(time_series)), labels=time_labels, rotation=45)
    if show_grid:
        plt.grid(True)
    plt.show()

def tokenize_text(text):
    text = re.sub(r'[^\w\s]', '', text).lower()
    tokens = text.split()
    stemmer = PorterStemmer()
    stemmed_tokens = [stemmer.stem(token) for token in tokens]
    return stemmed_tokens

def send_email(to_email, subject, body):
    server = smtplib.SMTP('smtp.example.com', 587)
    server.starttls()
    server.login('username', 'password')
    msg = MIMEMultipart()
    msg['From'] = 'from@example.com'
    msg['To'] = to_email
    msg['Subject'] = subject
    msg.attach(MIMEText(body, 'html'))
    server.send_message(msg)
    server.quit()

def fetch_movie_ratings(movie_id):
    if movie_id in cache:
        return cache[movie_id]
    url = f'http://api.example.com/movies/{movie_id}/ratings'
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        avg_rating = data['average_rating']
        num_reviews = data['number_of_reviews']
        cache[movie_id] = (avg_rating, num_reviews)
        return avg_rating, num_reviews
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_weather_data(location, start_date, end_date, fields=['daily_temperatures']):
    base_url = 'https://api.example.com/weather'
    params = {'location': location, 'start': start_date, 'end': end_date, 'page': 1}
    all_data = []
    while True:
        try:
            response = requests.get(base_url, params=params, timeout=5)
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            if not data.get('next_page'): break
            all_data.extend(data.get(fields[0], []))
            params['page'] += 1
        except (requests.RequestException, Exception) as e:
            return f'Error: {e}'
    return all_data

def process_large_file(file_path):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            for line in tqdm.tqdm(f):
                print(line.strip())
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def create_thumbnail(image_path, thumbnail_path):
    try:
        image = Image.open(image_path)
        image.thumbnail((200, 200))
        if image.format not in ['PNG', 'JPEG']:
            return 'Unsupported file format'
        image.save(thumbnail_path)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_stock_data(ticker, historical=False):

    if cache_key in cache:
        return cache[cache_key]
    url = f'https://api.example.com/stocks/{ticker}'
    params = {'historical': 'true'} if historical else {}
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=5)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        price = data['price']
        cache[cache_key] = price

    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def scrape_website(url):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    rate_limit = 1.0
    last_time = 0
    try:
        time_since_last_request = time.time() - last_time
        if time_since_last_request < rate_limit:
            time.sleep(rate_limit - time_since_last_request)
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, 'html.parser')
        titles = soup.find_all('h1')
        for title in titles:
            print(title.text)
        last_time = time.time()
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def sentiment_analysis(text):
    try:
        lang = detect(text)
        blob = TextBlob(text)
        if lang != 'en':
            blob = blob.translate(to='en')
        sentiment = blob.sentiment
        return {'polarity': sentiment.polarity, 'subjectivity': sentiment.subjectivity}
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def save_to_database(data):
    database = 'database.db'
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(database, timeout=10, check_same_thread=False)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        query = 'INSERT INTO table_name (column_name) VALUES (?)'
        cursor.execute(query, (data,))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    except Error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_user_data(user_id):
    try:
        username = quote_plus('user')
        password = quote_plus('password')
        uri = f'mongodb://{username}:{password}@localhost:27017/'
        client = pymongo.MongoClient(uri)
        db = client['user_db']
        collection = db['user_data']
        user = collection.find_one({'_id': user_id})
        if not user:
            return 'User not found'
        return user
    except PyMongoError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_url(url):
    rate_limit = 1.0
    last_time = 0
    try:
        time_since_last_request = time.time() - last_time
        if time_since_last_request < rate_limit:
            time.sleep(rate_limit - time_since_last_request)
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        last_time = time.time()
        return response.content
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def data_cleanup(df):
    if df.empty:
        return 'DataFrame is empty'
    df.fillna(0, inplace=True)
    if 'price' in df.columns:
        df['price'] = pd.to_numeric(df['price'], errors='coerce').fillna(0).astype(int)
    return df

def perform_clustering(data):
    scaler = StandardScaler()
    data_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(data)
    optimal_n_clusters = 3
    silhouette_avg = -1
    for n_clusters in range(2, 5):
        kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters).fit(data_scaled)
        silhouette_avg_n = silhouette_score(data_scaled, kmeans.labels_)
        if silhouette_avg_n > silhouette_avg:
            silhouette_avg = silhouette_avg_n
            optimal_n_clusters = n_clusters
    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=optimal_n_clusters).fit(data_scaled)
    return kmeans.labels_

def plot_graph(x, y):
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
    plt.plot(x, y, marker='o')
    for i, txt in enumerate(y):
        plt.annotate(txt, (x[i], y[i]))
    plt.xlabel('X-axis')
    plt.ylabel('Y-axis')
    plt.title('Plot Graph')
    plt.show()

def calculate_statistics(numbers):
    if not numbers:
        return 'List is empty'
    return {'mean': mean(numbers), 'median': median(numbers), 'mode': mode(numbers), 'stdev': stdev(numbers)}

def encrypt_message(message, key=None):
    if not key:
        key = Fernet.generate_key()
    cipher_suite = Fernet(key)
    try:
        encrypted_message = cipher_suite.encrypt(message.encode())
    except InvalidToken as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    return encrypted_message, key

def read_csv(file_path):
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        return 'File does not exist'
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            reader = csv.reader(f)
            next(reader, None)  # Skip header
            for row in reader:
                if row:  # Skip empty rows
                    print(row)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def extract_urls(text):
    pattern = r'https?://[\w.-]+(?:\.[\w.-]+)+([\w-./?%&=]*)?'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def write_to_file(file_path, content):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            lockf(f, LOCK_EX)
            f.write(content)
            lockf(f, LOCK_UN)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def download_image(url, path):
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        if 'image' not in response.headers.get('content-type', ''):
            return 'Not an image URL'
        with open(path, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(response.content)
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def parse_json_file(file_path, schema):
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        return 'File does not exist'
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        validate(instance=data, schema=schema)
        return data
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, jsonschema.ValidationError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_api_data(api_url):
    retries = 3
    delay = 1
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'custom-agent'}
    while retries > 0:
        try:
            response = requests.get(api_url, headers=headers, timeout=10)
            response.raise_for_status()
            return response.json()
        except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
            retries -= 1
            time.sleep(delay)
            delay *= 2
    return f'Error after {retries} retries'

def filter_dataframe(df, column='age', threshold=18):
    if not isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame):
        return 'Not a DataFrame'
    if column not in df.columns:
        return 'Invalid column name'
    df = df.dropna(subset=[column])
    return df[df[column] > threshold]

def train_model(X, y):
    try:
        scaler = StandardScaler()
        X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X)
        selector = SelectKBest(score_func=f_regression, k=5)
        X_selected = selector.fit_transform(X_scaled, y)
        model = LinearRegression()
        model.fit(X_selected, y)
        return model
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def calculate_mean(numbers):
    if not isinstance(numbers, (list, np.ndarray)) or not numbers:
        return 'Invalid input'
    try:
        numbers = np.array(numbers)
        q1 = np.percentile(numbers, 25)
        q3 = np.percentile(numbers, 75)
        iqr = q3 - q1
        lower_bound = q1 - (1.5 * iqr)
        upper_bound = q3 + (1.5 * iqr)
        filtered_numbers = numbers[(numbers >= lower_bound) & (numbers <= upper_bound)]
        return np.mean(filtered_numbers)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def convert_to_json(data):
    try:
        if not isinstance(data, (dict, list, tuple)):
            return 'Invalid data type'
        return json.dumps(data, indent=4, sort_keys=True)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_tweets(api, username):
    all_tweets = []
    last_tweet_id = None
    while True:
        try:
            if last_tweet_id:
                tweets = api.user_timeline(screen_name=username, count=200, max_id=last_tweet_id - 1)
            else:
                tweets = api.user_timeline(screen_name=username, count=200)
            if not tweets:
                break
            all_tweets.extend(tweets)
            last_tweet_id = tweets[-1].id
        except (RateLimitError, TweepError) as e:
            print(f'Error: {e}')
            break
    return [tweet.text for tweet in all_tweets]

def web_scraper(urls):
    def fetch_url(url):
        try:
            html = urllib.request.urlopen(url).read()
            return html
        except URLError as e:
            return f'Error: {e}'
    with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
        results = executor.map(fetch_url, urls)
    return list(results)

def encrypt_message(message, key):
    if not isinstance(message, bytes) or not isinstance(key, bytes):
        return 'Invalid input types'
    try:
        f = Fernet(key)
        encrypted = f.encrypt(message)
        return encrypted
    except InvalidToken as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def plot_data(x, y, xlabel='X-axis', ylabel='Y-axis', title='Plot'):
    if not all(isinstance(i, (int, float)) for i in x+y):
        return 'Invalid data types'
    try:
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
        plt.plot(x, y)
        plt.xlabel(xlabel)

def send_email(subject, body, to, attachment_path=None):
    try:
        server = smtplib.SMTP_SSL('smtp.gmail.com', 465)
        server.login('user', 'pass')
        msg = MIMEMultipart()
        msg['From'] = 'user'
        msg['To'] = to
        msg['Subject'] = subject
        msg.attach(MIMEText(body, 'plain'))
        if attachment_path:
            attachment = open(attachment_path, 'rb').read()
            msg.attach(MIMEText(attachment, 'base64'))
        server.sendmail('from', to, msg.as_string())
        server.quit()
    except SMTPException as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def download_pdfs(pdf_links):
    def fetch_pdf(link):
        try:
            filename = link.split('/')[-1]
            urllib.request.urlretrieve(link, filename, reporthook)
        except URLError as e:
            return f'Error: {e}'
    def reporthook(blocknum, blocksize, totalsize):
        readsofar = blocknum * blocksize
        percent = readsofar * 1e2 / totalsize
        print(f'{percent}% downloaded')
    with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
        results = executor.map(fetch_pdf, pdf_links)
    return list(results)

def save_to_csv(data, filename):
    try:
        if not all(isinstance(row, list) for row in data):
            return 'Invalid data format'
        with open(filename, 'w', newline='') as csvfile:
            fcntl.flock(csvfile, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            writer = csv.writer(csvfile)
            writer.writerows(data)
            fcntl.flock(csvfile, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def parse_xml(xml_string, schema_path):
    try:
        schema = XMLSchema(schema_path)
        if not schema.is_valid(xml_string):
            return 'Invalid XML against schema'
        root = ET.fromstring(xml_string)
        ns = {'ns': 'http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance'}
        for child in root.findall('.//ns:element', ns):
            print(child.tag, child.attrib)
    except ET.ParseError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_and_save_image(url, filename):
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, stream=True, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        image = Image.open(BytesIO(response.content))
        if image.format not in ['JPEG', 'PNG']:
            return 'Invalid image format'
        image.save(filename)
    except (HTTPError, Timeout, IOError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def get_weather_data(api_key, city):
    cache = {}
    if city in cache:
        return cache[city]
    try:
        time.sleep(1)  # Rate limiting
        url = f'http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather?q={city}&appid={api_key}'
        response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        weather_data = response.json()
        cache[city] = weather_data
        return weather_data
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def generate_thumbnail(image_path, thumbnail_path):
    try:
        with Image.open(image_path) as image:
            aspect_ratio = image.width / image.height
            new_size = (128, int(128 / aspect_ratio))
            image.thumbnail(new_size)
            image.save(thumbnail_path)
    except (FileNotFoundError, UnidentifiedImageError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def read_text_file(file_path, encoding='utf-8'):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding=encoding) as f:
            for line in f:
                yield line.strip()
    except (FileNotFoundError, UnicodeDecodeError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

    plt.xlabel(xlabel)

    plt.title(title)
    plt.show()

def create_zip(files, zip_name, compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED):
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_name, 'w', compression=compression) as zipf:
            for file in files:
                if not os.path.exists(file):
                    return f'{file} does not exist'
                zipf.write(file)
    except zipfile.BadZipFile as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def create_pandas_dataframe(data):
    try:
        df = pd.DataFrame(data, dtype=str)
        df.fillna('N/A', inplace=True)
        return df
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def calculate_statistics(numbers):
    try:
        mean = sum(numbers) / len(numbers)
        min_val = min(numbers)
        max_val = max(numbers)
        med = median(numbers)
        mod = mode(numbers)
        outliers = [x for x in numbers if x < mean - 2 * mod or x > mean + 2 * mod]
        return {'mean': mean, 'min': min_val, 'max': max_val, 'median': med, 'mode': mod, 'outliers': outliers}
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def sort_list(lst, key_func=None):
    try:
        filtered_lst = [x for x in lst if x is not None]
        sorted_lst = sorted(filtered_lst, key=key_func) if key_func else sorted(filtered_lst)
        return sorted_lst
    except TypeError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_api_data(api_url, page=1):
    rate_limit = 1.0
    last_time = 0
    try:
        time_since_last_request = time.time() - last_time
        if time_since_last_request < rate_limit:
            time.sleep(rate_limit - time_since_last_request)
        response = requests.get(f'{api_url}?page={page}')
        last_time = time.time()
        return response.json()
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

fetch_api_data(api_url, page)

def search_text(pattern, text):
    try:
        optimized_pattern = r'\b' + re.escape(pattern) + r'\b'
        return re.findall(optimized_pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)
    except re.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def read_csv(file_path):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            result = chardet.detect(f.read())
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding=result['encoding']) as f:
            reader = csv.Sniffer().sniff(f.read(1024))
            f.seek(0)
            reader = csv.reader(f, delimiter=reader.delimiter)
            next(reader, None)
            for row in reader:
                print(row)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def retry_if_error(exception):
    return isinstance(exception, requests.RequestException)

def get_weather_data(api_key, city):
    try:
        response = requests.get(f'http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather?q={city}&appid={api_key}')
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        if 'weather' in data and data['weather']:
            return data['weather'][0]['description']
        return 'No weather data available'
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fibonacci(n):
    if n == 0:
        return 0
    elif n == 1:
        return 1
    else:
        return fibonacci(n-1) + fibonacci(n-2)

def write_to_file(file_path, text):
    try:
        encoding = chardet.detect(text.encode())['encoding']
        with open(file_path, 'w', encoding=encoding) as f:
            fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            f.write(text)
            fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def deep_merge(dict1, dict2):
    for k, v in dict2.items():
        if k in dict1 and isinstance(dict1[k], dict) and isinstance(v, dict):
            deep_merge(dict1[k], v)
        else:
            dict1[k] = v

def merge_dictionaries(dict1, dict2):
    merged_dict = defaultdict(dict)
    deep_merge(merged_dict, dict1)
    deep_merge(merged_dict, dict2)
    return dict(merged_dict)

def upload_file_to_s3(file_path, bucket_name, metadata=None):
    try:
        s3 = boto3.client('s3')
        def progress_callback(bytes_transferred):
            print(f'{bytes_transferred} bytes transferred')
        s3.upload_file(file_path, bucket_name, file_path.split('/')[-1], Callback=progress_callback, ExtraArgs={'Metadata': metadata})
    except (NoCredentialsError, PartialCredentialsError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def api_data_extraction(api_url, per_page=50):
    all_data = []
    page = 1
    rate_limit = 1.0
    last_time = 0
    try:
        while True:
            time_since_last_request = time.time() - last_time
            if time_since_last_request < rate_limit:
                time.sleep(rate_limit - time_since_last_request)
            response = requests.get(f'{api_url}?page={page}&per_page={per_page}', timeout=10)
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            if not data:
                break
            all_data.extend(data)
            page += 1
            last_time = time.time()
        return all_data
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def data_preprocessing(df):
    try:
        df.dropna(inplace=True)
        df = df.apply(lambda x: x.str.lower() if x.dtype == 'object' else x)
        num_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=['float64', 'int64']).columns
        scaler = StandardScaler()
        df[num_cols] = scaler.fit_transform(df[num_cols])
        df = df.convert_dtypes()
        return df
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def user_authentication(username, password):
    hashed_password = hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
    if username in failed_attempts and time.time() - failed_attempts[username]['timestamp'] < 60:
        return 'Account locked'
    if username in user_db:
        if hashed_password == user_db[username]:
            return 'Authenticated'
        else:
            failed_attempts[username] = {'count': failed_attempts.get(username, {'count': 0})['count'] + 1, 'timestamp': time.time()}
            if failed_attempts[username]['count'] >= 3:
                return 'Account locked'
            return 'Authentication failed'
    else:
        return 'User not found'

def image_processing(image_path, output_format='PNG', enhance_factor=1.0):
    try:
        img = Image.open(image_path)
        img = img.resize((300, 300))
        enhancer = ImageEnhance.Contrast(img)
        img = enhancer.enhance(enhance_factor)
        img.save(f'resized_image.{output_format}', format=output_format)
    except UnidentifiedImageError as e:
        return f'Error: Unsupported image format {e}'

def audio_processing(audio_path, output_format='wav'):
    try:
        audio_data, samplerate = sf.read(audio_path)
        audio_data = nr.reduce_noise(audio_clip=audio_data, noise_clip=audio_data)
        output_path = f'processed_audio.{output_format}'
        sf.write(output_path, audio_data, samplerate, subtype=splitext(output_path)[1][1:].upper())
    except RuntimeError as e:
        return f'Error: Invalid audio file {e}'

        server.login(from_email, password)
        server.sendmail(from_email, to_email, f'Subject: {subject}\n{body}')

    except (SMTPException, SMTPAuthenticationError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def twitter_bot(api_key, api_secret, tweet):
    rate_limit = 1.0
    last_time = 0
    try:
        auth = tweepy.OAuthHandler(api_key, api_secret)
        api = tweepy.API(auth)
        if len(tweet) > 280:
            return 'Tweet exceeds character limit'
        time_since_last_request = time.time() - last_time
        if time_since_last_request < rate_limit:
            time.sleep(rate_limit - time_since_last_request)
        api.update_status(status=tweet)
        last_time = time.time()
    except (TweepError, RateLimitError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def text_summary(text):
    try:
        if len(re.findall(r'\w+', text)) < 40:
            return 'Text too short for summarization'
        summary = summarize(text)
        key_words = keywords(text)
        return {'summary': summary, 'keywords': key_words.split('\n')}
    except ValueError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def video_processing(video_path, output_format='avi'):
    try:
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
        fourcc = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*'XVID')
        out = cv2.VideoWriter(f'output.{output_format}', fourcc, 20.0, (640,480))
        while cap.isOpened():
            ret, frame = cap.read()
            if not ret:
                break
            out.write(frame)
            cv2.imshow('Video', frame)
            if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord('q'):
                break
        cap.release()
        out.release()
        cv2.destroyAllWindows()
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def pdf_parser(file_path, search_term=None):
    try:
        pdf = PdfFileReader(file_path)
        if pdf.isEncrypted:
            return 'Encrypted PDF, unable to parse'
        text = ''
        metadata = pdf.getDocumentInfo()
        for i in range(pdf.getNumPages()):
            text += pdf.getPage(i).extractText()
        if search_term:
            return {'text': text, 'metadata': metadata, 'search_term_occurrences': text.count(search_term)}
        return {'text': text, 'metadata': metadata}
    except PdfReadError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def image_classification(image_path, model_type='VGG16', confidence_threshold=0.5):
    try:
        img = image.load_img(image_path, target_size=(224, 224))
        x = image.img_to_array(img)
        x = preprocess_input(x)
        if model_type == 'VGG16':
            model = VGG16(weights='imagenet')
        elif model_type == 'ResNet50':
            model = ResNet50(weights='imagenet')
        preds = model.predict(x)
        decoded_preds = decode_predictions(preds, top=3)[0]
        return [pred for pred in decoded_preds if pred[2] >= confidence_threshold]
    except UnidentifiedImageError as e:
        return f'Error: Unsupported image type {e}'

def data_encryption(text, key_path='key.key'):
    try:
        key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(Fernet.generate_key())
        with open(key_path, 'wb') as key_file:
            key_file.write(key)
        cipher_suite = Fernet(key)
        encrypted_text = cipher_suite.encrypt(text.encode())
        return encrypted_text
    except InvalidToken as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def download_audio_from_youtube(url, bitrate='128kbps', output_name='audio'):
    try:
        yt = YouTube(url)
        stream = yt.streams.filter(only_audio=True, file_extension='mp4').order_by('abr').first()
        if stream.abr != bitrate:
            stream = yt.streams.filter(only_audio=True, abr=bitrate).first()
        if not stream:
            return 'No matching audio stream found'
        stream.download(filename=output_name)
    except exceptions.PytubeError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def audio_transcription(audio_path, language='en-US'):
    recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
    try:
        with sr.AudioFile(audio_path) as source:
            recognizer.adjust_for_ambient_noise(source)
            audio_data = recognizer.record(source)
        text = recognizer.recognize_google(audio_data, language=language)
        return text
    except sr.UnknownValueError:
        return 'Could not understand audio'
    except sr.RequestError as e:
        return f'Could not request results; {e}'

def calculate_statistics(data):
    try:
        if len(data) == 0:
            return 'Data array is empty'
        mean = np.mean(data)
        median = np.median(data)
        std = np.std(data)
        skewness = skew(data)
        kurt = kurtosis(data)
        return {'mean': mean, 'median': median, 'std': std, 'skewness': skewness, 'kurtosis': kurt}
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def download_file(url, filename):
    try:
        with requests.get(url, stream=True) as r:
            r.raise_for_status()
            total_size = int(r.headers.get('content-length', 0))
            with open(filename, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=8192):
                    f.write(chunk)
        return 'Download successful'
    except (HTTPError, Timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def extract_zip(zip_path, output_path, password=None, specific_files=None):
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'r') as zip_ref:
            if password:
                zip_ref.setpassword(password.encode())
            if specific_files:
                for file in specific_files:
                    zip_ref.extract(file, output_path)
            else:
                zip_ref.extractall(output_path)
        return 'Extraction successful'
    except zipfile.BadZipFile as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def text_translation(text, target_lang='en'):
    try:
        if len(text) > 5000:
            return 'Text exceeds the 5000 character limit'
        translator = Translator()
        detected_lang = translator.detect(text).lang
        if detected_lang == target_lang:
            return 'Source and target languages are the same'
        translated = translator.translate(text, dest=target_lang)
        return translated.text
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

        clip.audio.write_audiofile(audio_path, codec=audio_format, fps=sample_rate)
        return 'Audio extracted successfully'
    except MoviepyError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def nlp_pipeline(text):
    try:
        nlp = spacy.load('en_core_web_sm')
        doc = nlp(text)
        tokens = [token.text for token in doc]
        pos_tags = [token.pos_ for token in doc]
        ner_tags = [(ent.text, ent.label_) for ent in doc.ents]
        dependencies = [(token.text, token.dep_) for token in doc]
        return {'tokens': tokens, 'pos_tags': pos_tags, 'ner_tags': ner_tags, 'dependencies': dependencies}
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def get_top_n_words(text, n):
    try:
        text = re.sub(r'[^\w\s]', '', text).lower()
        words = text.split()
        counter = Counter(words)
        return counter.most_common(n)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def quick_sort(arr):
    if len(arr) <= 1:
        return arr
    pivot = arr[len(arr) // 2]
    left = [x for x in arr if x < pivot]
    middle = [x for x in arr if x == pivot]
    right = [x for x in arr if x > pivot]
    return quick_sort(left) + middle + quick_sort(right)

def split(mystring, separator):
    if not isinstance(mystring, str) or not isinstance(separator, str):
        return 'Error: Both arguments must be strings'
    return mystring.split(separator)

def get_day_of_week(date):
    if not isinstance(date, datetime):
        return 'Error: Argument must be a datetime object'
    return date.strftime("%A")

def list_to_string(input_list, separator):
    if not all(isinstance(i, str) for i in input_list):
        return 'Error: All elements in the list must be strings'
    return separator.join(input_list)

def generate_password():
    characters = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEF(...redacted...)=[ ]{}:'
    password = random.choices(characters, k = 16)
    return ''.join(password)

def f(x):
    """Returns -1 if x is greater than 0, otherwise returns 1"""
    if x > 0:
        return -1
    else:
        return 1

def median(arr):
    if len(arr) == 0:
        return None
    sortedArr = sorted(arr)
    length = len(arr)
    if length % 2 == 0:
        return ((sortedArr[length//2-1] + sortedArr[length//2])/2)
    else:
        return sortedArr[length//2]

def sum_of_list(my_list):
    if not all(isinstance(i, (int, float)) for i in my_list):
        return 'Error: All elements in the list must be numbers'
    sum = 0
    for number in my_list:
        sum = sum + number
    return sum

def first_non_repeating_char(input_str):
    if not isinstance(input_str, str):
        return 'Error: Input must be a string'
    char_count = {}
    for ch in input_str:
        if ch in char_count:
            char_count[ch] += 1
        else:
            char_count[ch] = 1
    for ch in input_str:
        if char_count[ch] == 1:
            return ch
    return None

def generate_random_number():
    return random.randint(100000000000000, 999999999999999)

def generate_random_string():
    return ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_letters + string.digits, k=12))

def print_pattern():
    for i in range(1,6):
        for j in range(1,i+1):
            print("*", end=" ")
        print()

def is_anagram(word1, word2):
    if not isinstance(word1, str) or not isinstance(word2, str):
        return 'Error: Both arguments must be strings'
    return sorted(word1) == sorted(word2)

def create_2D_array(rows, columns):
    arr = [[0 for _ in range(columns)] for _ in range(rows)]
    return arr

def replace_substring(string, substring, replacement):
    if not isinstance(string, str) or not isinstance(substring, str) or not isinstance(replacement, str):
        return 'Error: All arguments must be strings'
    return string.replace(substring, replacement)

def count_chars(string):
    if not isinstance(string, str):
        return 'Error: Input must be a string'
    char_occurrences = {}
    for char in string:
        if char not in char_occurrences:
            char_occurrences[char] = 1
        else:
            char_occurrences[char] += 1
    return char_occurrences

def generate_primes(n):
    primes = []
    i = 2
    while len(primes) < n:
        for p in primes:
            if i % p == 0:
                break
        else:
            primes.append(i)
        i += 1
    return primes

def common_elements(list1, list2):
    return list(set(list1) & set(list2))

def longest_palindrome(string):
    if not isinstance(string, str):
        return 'Error: Input must be a string'
    palindrome = ''
    for i in range(len(string)):
        for j in range(i+1, len(string)+1):
            substring = string[i:j]
            if substring == substring[::-1] and len(substring) > len(palindrome):
                palindrome = substring
    return palindrome

def intersection(list1, list2):
    return list(set(list1) & set(list2))

def isArmstrongNumber(num):
    if not isinstance(num, int):
        return 'Error: Input must be an integer'
    sum = 0
    temp = num
    while temp > 0:
        digit = temp % 10
        sum += digit ** 3
        temp //= 10
    if num == sum:
        return True
    else:
        return False

def print_str_list(in_list):
    if not isinstance(in_list, list):
        return 'Error: Input must be a list'
    return " ".join(str(x) for x in in_list)

        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.salary = salary

def factorial(n):
 if not isinstance(n, int) or n < 0:
 return 'Error: Input must be a non-negative integer'
 if n == 0:
 return 1
 else: 
 return n * factorial(n-1)

def mmddyyyy2ddmmyyyy(date_string):
 if len(date_string) != 10 or date_string[2] != '/' or date_string[5] != '/':
 raise ValueError('Invalid date format')
 mm, dd, yyyy = date_string.split('/')
 new_date_string = '{}-{}-{}'.format(dd, mm, yyyy)
 return new_date_string

def is_prime(num):
 if num <= 1: 
 return False
 if num == 2:
 return True
 if num % 2 == 0:
 return False
 for i in range(3, int(num**0.5) + 1, 2): 
 if num % i == 0: 
 return False
 return True

# 1. Optimized the prime number check by checking only up to the square root of the number and skipping even numbers.
# Code fixed. # Executing code.

def longest_common_prefix(arr_of_dictionaries):
 if len(arr_of_dictionaries) == 0:
 return ''
 keys = [set(d.keys()) for d in arr_of_dictionaries]
 common_keys = set.intersection(*keys)
 prefix = ''
 for key in common_keys:
 if key.startswith(prefix):
 prefix = key
 return prefix

def removeChars(string, charset):
 return ''.join(c for c in string if c not in charset)

def common_items(arr1, arr2):
 return list(set(arr1) & set(arr2))

def all_unique(string):
 return len(string) == len(set(string))

def greatest_difference(numbers_list):
    return max(numbers_list) - min(numbers_list)

class Stack: 
    def __init__(self): 
        self.stack = []

    def push(self,element): 
        self.stack.append(element)  

    def pop(self): 
        if not self.stack:
            return 'Error: Stack is empty'
        return self.stack.pop()

    def peek(self):
        if not self.stack:
            return 'Error: Stack is empty'
        return self.stack[-1]

def unique_words(sentence):
    return set(sentence.split())

def letter_counts(sentence):

def is_anagram(str1, str2): 
 if not isinstance(str1, str) or not isinstance(str2, str):
 return 'Error: Both inputs must be strings'
 if len(str1) != len(str2): 
 return False 
 
 s1 = sorted(str1) 
 s2 = sorted(str2) 
 
 return s1 == s2

def generate_random_string(length=5):
 return ''.join(random.choice(string.ascii_letters) for _ in range(length))

def build_model(X, y):
    X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(X, y, test_size=0.2, random_state=0)
    model = LinearRegression()
    model.fit(X_train, y_train)
    y_pred = model.predict(X_test)
    print('Mean squared error: %.2f' % mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred))
    print('Coefficient of determination: %.2f' % r2_score(y_test, y_pred))

def generate_password(length=8):
 chars = string.ascii_letters + string.digits
 return ''.join(random.choice(chars) for _ in range(length))

def generate_random_array(n):
 return [random.randint(1, 100) for _ in range(n)]

def create_dict(*args):
 return dict(zip(args[::2], [{'age': age, 'hobby': hobby} for age, hobby in zip(args[1::2], args[2::2])]))

 if not all(isinstance(row, list) for row in arr):
 return 'Error: Input is not a 2D array'
 for row in arr:
 for element in row:
 print(element, end=' ')
 print()

def flatten(nums):
 if not isinstance(nums, list):
 return 'Error: Input is not a list'
 flattened_list = []
 for elem in nums:
 if isinstance(elem, list):
 flattened_list.extend(flatten(elem))
 else:
 flattened_list.append(elem)
 return flattened_list

def print_max_element_idx(arr):
 if not arr:
 return 'Error: Array is empty'
 max_num = arr[0]
 max_idx = 0
 for i in range(1, len(arr)):
 if arr[i] > max_num:
 max_idx = i
 max_num = arr[i]
 return max_idx

def most_frequent_element(arr):
 if not arr:
 return 'Error: Array is empty'
 counter = Counter(arr)
 return counter.most_common(1)[0][0]

def clean_data(data):
    for item in data:
        item['age'] = int(item['age']) if item['age'] else None
        item['income'] = int(item['income'].replace('$', '').replace(',', '')) if item['income'] else None
    return data

def sum_list(some_list):
    if not isinstance(some_list, list):
        return 'Error: Input is not a list'
    result = 0
    for num in some_list:
        result += num
    return result

def pop_element(lst):
    try:
        return lst.pop()
    except IndexError:
        return 'Error: List is empty'

def SortArray(array):
    if not isinstance(array, list):
        return 'Error: Input is not a list'
    for i in range(len(array)):
        for j in range(len(array) - i - 1):
            if array[j] > array[j + 1]:
                array[j], array[j + 1] = array[j + 1], array[j]
    return array

def remove_substring(string, substring):
    if not isinstance(string, str) or not isinstance(substring, str):
        return 'Error: Input is not a string'
    return string.replace(substring, '')

def generateDict(n):
    if not isinstance(n, int):
        return 'Error: Input is not an integer'
    return {i: i**2 for i in range(1, n+1)}

 lst.remove(element)
 return lst

def multiply_matrices(matrixA, matrixB): 
 if not all(isinstance(row, list) for row in matrixA) or not all(isinstance(row, list) for row in matrixB):
 return 'Error: Input is not a 2D array'
 result_matrix = [[0 for _ in range(len(matrixB[0]))] for _ in range(len(matrixA))]

 for i in range(len(matrixA)): 
 for j in range(len(matrixB[0])): 
 for k in range(len(matrixB)): 
 result_matrix[i][j] += matrixA[i][k] * matrixB[k][j] 
 
 return result_matrix

 return 'Error: Input is not a set'

def find_dates(text):
    pattern = '\b(19[0-9]{2}|20[0-1][0-9]|202[0-3])\b'
    years = re.findall(pattern, text)
    pattern = '\b(0[1-9]|1[0-2])\b'
    months = re.findall(pattern, text)
    return {'years': years, 'months': months}

def find_urls(text):
    pattern = 'https?://(?:[a-zA-Z]|[0-9]|[$-_@.&+]|[!*\\(\\),]|(?:%[0-9a-fA-F][0-9a-fA-F]))+'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def find_prices(text):
    pattern = r'\\\$([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]{2})?)'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def Fibonacci(n): 
 if not isinstance(n, int):
 return 'Error: Input is not an integer'
 if n<0: 
 print("Incorrect input") 
 elif n==1: 
 return 0
 elif n==2: 
 return 1
 else: 
 return Fibonacci(n-1)+Fibonacci(n-2)

def get_temperature():
 url = 'http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather?q=New York City,NY&units=imperial'
 response = requests.get(url)
 if response.status_code != 200:
 return 'Error: Failed to retrieve data'
 data = response.json()
    current_temperature = data['main']['temp']
    return current_temperature

class Customer:
    def __init__(self, name, address, email):
        if not all(isinstance(i, str) for i in [name, address, email]):
            raise ValueError('All inputs must be strings')
        self.name = name
        self.address = address
        self.email = email

    def get_name(self):
        return self.name

    def set_name(self, name):
        self.name = name

    def get_address(self):
        return self.address

    def set_address(self, address):
        self.address = address

    def get_email(self):
        return self.email

    def set_email(self, email):
        self.email = email

def shuffleArray(arr):
    if not isinstance(arr, list):
        return 'Error: Input is not a list'
    temp_arr = arr.copy()
    for i in range(len(temp_arr)):
        j = random.randint(0, len(temp_arr)-1)
        temp_arr[i], temp_arr[j] = temp_arr[j], temp_arr[i]
    return temp_arr

def open_notepad():
    if platform.system() == 'Windows':
        try:
            shell32 = ctypes.windll.shell32
            if hasattr(shell32, 'ShellExecuteW'):
                shell32.ShellExecuteW(None, 'open', 'notepad.exe', None, None, 1)
            else:
                return 'ShellExecuteW not found'
        except AttributeError:
            return 'Method not found'
    else:
        return 'Unsupported platform'

def list_files():
    try:
        output = os.popen('ls -alh').read()
        return output
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def calculate_sum(data):
    try:
        json_data = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(json_data, list):
            return 'Invalid JSON format'
        return sum(json_data)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def set_encoder(obj):
    if isinstance(obj, set):
        return list(obj)
    raise TypeError(f'Object of type {obj.__class__.__name__} is not JSON serializable')

def find_max_value(data):
    try:
        result = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(result, list) or not result:
            return 'Invalid or empty list'
        return max(result)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def write_json_to_file(list_data, file_path):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'w') as file:
            json.dump(list_data, file)
    except IOError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def filter_data(data):
    try:
        json_data = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(json_data, list):
            return 'Invalid JSON format'
        return [x for x in json_data if x > 1]
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def save_pretty_json(list_of_dicts, file_path):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(list_of_dicts, f, indent=4)
    except IOError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def calculate_sum_from_json(data):
    try:
        json_list = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(json_list, list):
            return 'Invalid JSON format'
        return sum(json_list)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

    except IOError as e:

def find_titles(text):
    pattern = r'\b(?:Mr|Mrs|Ms|Dr)\.'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def find_years_and_months(text):
    pattern = r'\b(?:19[0-9]{2}|20[0-9]{2}|0[1-9]|1[0-2])\b'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def find_vowels(text):
    pattern = r'(?i)[aeiou]'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def find_ssn(text):
    pattern = r'\b\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}\b'
    return re.findall(pattern, text)

def find_key_value_pairs(text):
    pattern = r'([\w\s]+)\s*:\s*([\w\s]+)'
    return dict(re.findall(pattern, text))

    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r') as file:
            return json.load(file)
    except (IOError, json.JSONDecodeError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def run_command(command):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=10)
        return result.stdout.decode('utf-8'), result.stderr.decode('utf-8')
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
        return f'Timeout: {e}'

def launch_app(app):
    try:
        subprocess.Popen([app])
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def run_command_in_background(command):
    try:
        subprocess.Popen(command)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def run_command_safely(command):
    if 'rm' in command or 'format' in command:
        return 'Dangerous command detected'
    try:
        subprocess.run(command)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def ping_host(host):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(['ping', host], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=5)
        return result.stdout.decode('utf-8'), result.stderr.decode('utf-8')
    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, subprocess.CalledProcessError) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def list_files():
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(['ls', '-alh'], stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
        return result.stdout.decode('utf-8')
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def run_as_user(command, user):
    try:
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-u', user] + command, stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def run_python_script(script):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(['python3', script], check=True)
        return result.returncode
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def grep_txt_files():
    try:
        ls = subprocess.Popen(['ls', '-alh'], stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
        grep = subprocess.Popen(['grep', 'txt'], stdin=ls.stdout, stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
        ls.stdout.close()
        return grep.communicate()[0].decode('utf-8')
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_url(url):
    try:
        subprocess.run(['curl', '-L', url], timeout=10)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
        return f'Timeout: {e}'

def create_directory(dir_name):
    if not os.path.exists(dir_name):
        os.mkdir(dir_name)
    else:
        return 'Directory already exists'

def change_directory(path):
    try:
        os.chdir(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return f'Invalid path: {path}'

def get_home_directory():
    try:
        return os.environ.get('HOME', 'Environment variable not found')
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def delete_file(file_name):
    if os.path.exists(file_name):
        os.remove(file_name)
    else:
        return 'File does not exist'

def rename_file(old_name, new_name):
    if os.path.exists(old_name):
        if not os.path.exists(new_name):
            os.rename(old_name, new_name)
        else:
            return 'New file name already exists'
    else:
        return 'Old file does not exist'

def remove_directory(dir_name):
    if os.path.exists(dir_name):
        if not os.listdir(dir_name):
            os.rmdir(dir_name)
        else:
            return 'Directory is not empty'
    else:
        return 'Directory does not exist'

def create_new_file(file_name):
    try:
        with open(file_name, 'w') as f:
            f.write('')
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def join_path(*args):
    cleaned_args = [str(arg).strip() for arg in args if arg]
    if cleaned_args:
        return os.path.join(*cleaned_args)
    else:
        return 'Invalid path components'

def create_symlink(target, link):
    if os.path.exists(target):
        if not os.path.exists(link):
            os.symlink(target, link)
        else:
            return 'Link already exists'
    else:
        return 'Target file does not exist'

def allocate_memory():
    MEM_COMMIT = 0x1000
    PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE = 0x40
    try:
        pVoid = wintypes.LPVOID()
        result = ctypes.windll.kernel32.VirtualAlloc(pVoid, 1024, MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)
        if result is None:
            return 'Memory allocation failed'
    except AttributeError:
        return 'Method not found'

def change_wallpaper(image_path):
    SPI_SETDESKWALLPAPER = 20
    if platform.system() == 'Windows':
        try:
            result = ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(SPI_SETDESKWALLPAPER, 0, image_path, 3)
            if result == 0:
                return 'Failed to set wallpaper'
        except AttributeError:
            return 'Method not found'
    else:
        return 'Unsupported platform'

def custom_sin(value):
    if platform.system() == 'Linux':
        try:
            libm = ctypes.CDLL('libm.so.6')
            if hasattr(libm, 'sin'):
                return libm.sin(ctypes.c_double(value))
            else:
                return 'sin function not found'
        except OSError:
            return 'libm.so.6 could not be loaded'
    else:
        return 'Unsupported platform'

def change_permissions(file_name, permissions):
    if os.path.exists(file_name):
        try:
            os.chmod(file_name, int(permissions, 8))
        except ValueError:
            return 'Invalid permission format'
    else:
        return 'File not found'

def remove_file(file_name):
    if os.path.exists(file_name):
        os.remove(file_name)
    else:
        return 'File does not exist'

def set_user_id(uid):
    if hasattr(os, 'setuid'):
        try:
            os.setuid(uid)
        except PermissionError:
            return 'Permission denied'
    else:
        return 'setuid not supported'

def get_env_var(var_name):
    return os.environ.get(var_name, 'Environment variable not set')

def get_computer_name():
    buffer = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(15)
    size = wintypes.DWORD(15)
    try:
        result = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetComputerNameW(buffer, ctypes.byref(size))
        if result == 0:
            return 'Failed to get computer name'
        else:
            return buffer.value
    except AttributeError:
        return 'Method not found'

def rename_file(old_name, new_name):
    if os.path.exists(old_name):
        if not os.path.exists(new_name):
            os.rename(old_name, new_name)
        else:
            return 'Destination file already exists'
    else:
        return 'Source file does not exist'

def list_directory():
    try:
        subprocess.run(['ls'], check=True)
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def get_current_user():
    try:
        return os.getlogin()
    except OSError:
        return getpass.getuser()

def create_nested_directory(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        os.makedirs(path)
    else:
        return 'Directory already exists'

def remove_directory(path):
    if os.path.exists(path):
        shutil.rmtree(path)
    else:
        return 'Directory does not exist'

def show_message_box(message, title):
    try:
        if platform.system() == 'Windows':
            ctypes.windll.user32.MessageBoxW(0, message, title, 0)
        elif platform.system() == 'Linux':
            ctypes.CDLL('libX11.so').XmbSetWMProperties(message, title, None, None, None, 0, None, None, None)
    except AttributeError:
        return 'DLL method not found'
    except OSError:
        return 'DLL could not be loaded'

def custom_printf(message):
    if platform.system() == 'Linux':
        try:
            libc = ctypes.CDLL('libc.so.6')
            printf = libc.printf
            printf(message)
        except AttributeError:
            return 'Function not found'
    else:
        return 'Unsupported platform'

def get_last_error():
    try:
        error_code = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetLastError()
        if error_code != 0:
            return WinError(error_code)
        else:
            return 'No errors'
    except AttributeError:
        return 'Method not found'

def call_some_function():
    try:
        lib = ctypes.CDLL('somedll.dll')
        if hasattr(lib, 'some_function'):
            func = lib.some_function
            func()
        else:
            return 'Function not found in the library'
    except OSError:
        return 'Library could not be loaded'

def custom_beep(frequency, duration):
    try:
        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.LoadLibrary('Kernel32.dll')
        if hasattr(kernel32, 'Beep'):
            kernel32.Beep(frequency, duration)
        else:
            return 'Beep function not found'
    except OSError:
        return 'Kernel32.dll could not be loaded'

class CustomDataset(Dataset):
    texts: list
    labels: list
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.texts)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        return {'text': self.texts[idx], 'label': self.labels[idx]}

def collate_fn(batch):
    texts = [item['text'] for item in batch]
    labels = [item['label'] for item in batch]
    encoding = tokenizer(texts, padding=True, truncation=True, return_tensors='pt')
    return {'input_ids': encoding['input_ids'], 'attention_mask': encoding['attention_mask'], 'labels': torch.tensor(labels)}

def post_process_translation(translation_batch):
    return [t.replace('<pad>', '').strip() for t in translation_batch]

def train_step(batch):
    model.zero_grad()
    outputs = model(**batch)
    loss = outputs.loss
    loss.backward()
    torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

def summarize(text, min_length=30, max_length=100):
    inputs = tokenizer.encode('summarize: ' + text, return_tensors='pt', max_length=512, truncation=True)
    summary_ids = model.generate(inputs, num_beams=4, min_length=min_length, max_length=max_length, early_stopping=True)
    summary = tokenizer.decode(summary_ids[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
    return summary

def sign_message(message, private_key):
    h = SHA256.new(message)
    signature = pkcs1_15.new(private_key).sign(h)
    return signature

def verify_signature(message, signature, public_key):
    h = SHA256.new(message)
    try:
        pkcs1_15.new(public_key).verify(h, signature)
        return True
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False

def encrypt_with_padding(data, key):
    cipher = DES.new(key, DES.MODE_CBC)
    padded_data = pad(data, DES.block_size)
    encrypted_data = cipher.encrypt(padded_data)
    return cipher.iv, encrypted_data

def decrypt_with_padding(iv, encrypted_data, key):
    cipher = DES.new(key, DES.MODE_CBC, iv=iv)
    padded_data = cipher.decrypt(encrypted_data)
    return unpad(padded_data, DES.block_size)

def start_server(host='localhost', port=8080):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.bind((host, port))
        s.listen()
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            with conn:
                print(f'Connected by {addr}')
                try:
                    while True:
                        data = conn.recv(1024)
                        if not data:
                            break
                        conn.sendall(data)
                except ConnectionResetError:
                    print('Client disconnected')

def udp_communicate(host='localhost', port=8080):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM) as s:
        s.settimeout(5.0)
        try:
            s.sendto(b'Hello, Server!', (host, port))
            data, addr = s.recvfrom(1024)
            print(f'Received {data} from {addr}')
        except socket.timeout:
            print('Timeout, no response from server')

def tcp_client(host='localhost', port=8080):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.connect((host, port))
        while True:
            msg = input('Enter message: ')
            s.sendall(msg.encode())
            data = s.recv(1024)
            print(f'Received: {data.decode()}')
            if msg.lower() == 'quit':
                break

def connect_to_host(hostname='www.google.com', port=80):
    addr_info = socket.getaddrinfo(hostname, port, family=socket.AF_INET, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    addr = addr_info[0][-1]
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.connect(addr)
        print(f'Connected to {hostname} on {addr}')

def smooth_move(x, y, steps=10):
    cur_x, cur_y = win32api.GetCursorPos()
    dx = (x - cur_x) / steps
    dy = (y - cur_y) / steps
    for i in range(steps):
        cur_x += dx
        cur_y += dy
        win32api.SetCursorPos((int(cur_x), int(cur_y)))
        time.sleep(0.01)

def load_image(name):
    try:
        image = pygame.image.load(name)
        return image.convert()
    except pygame.error as e:
        print(f'Could not load image: {e}')
        return None

def play_sound(sound_file, volume=0.5):
    pygame.mixer.init()
    pygame.mixer.music.load(sound_file)
    pygame.mixer.music.set_volume(volume)
    pygame.mixer.music.play(-1)

def render_text(text, font_size=36, color=(255, 255, 255)):
    font = pygame.font.Font(None, font_size)
    text_surface = font.render(text, True, color)
    return text_surface, text_surface.get_rect()

def get_combinations(lst, n, mode='combinations', repeat=False):
    if mode == 'combinations':
        return list(itertools.combinations_with_replacement(lst, n) if repeat else itertools.combinations(lst, n))
    elif mode == 'permutations':
        return list(itertools.permutations(lst, n))
    return 'Invalid mode'

def cartesian_product(filter_fn=None, *args):
    if filter_fn:
        return list(filter(filter_fn, itertools.product(*args)))
    return list(itertools.product(*args))

def get_cycle(iterable, n):
    return list(itertools.islice(itertools.cycle(iterable), n))

def unique_elements(iterable, key_fn=None):
    return list(itertools.unique_everseen(iterable, key=key_fn))

def chunk_data(data, n, fill_value=None):
    args = [iter(data)] * n
    return list(itertools.zip_longest(*args, fillvalue=fill_value))

def calculate_area(shape, **kwargs):
    if shape == 'circle':
        return math.pi * kwargs.get('radius', 0) ** 2
    elif shape == 'square':
        return kwargs.get('side', 0) ** 2
    elif shape == 'rectangle':
        return kwargs.get('length', 0) * kwargs.get('width', 0)
    elif shape == 'triangle':
        return 0.5 * kwargs.get('base', 0) * kwargs.get('height', 0)
    return 'Invalid shape'

def find_pattern(pattern, text):
    optimized_pattern = re.compile(r'a[\w]b')
    return optimized_pattern.findall(text)

def safe_sqrt(x):
    if x < 0:
        return cmath.sqrt(x)
    else:
        return math.sqrt(x)

def current_time_with_timezone(tz='UTC'):
    utc_now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
    target_tz = pytz.timezone(tz)
    localized_time = utc_now.astimezone(target_tz)
    return localized_time

        sys.exit(0)
    for arg in args:
        print(f'Processing argument: {arg}')

def read_file(filename):
    try:
        with open(filename, 'r') as file:
            content = file.read()
        print(content)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        logging.error(f'File {filename} not found.')
        sys.exit(1)
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f'An error occurred: {e}')
        sys.exit(1)

def check_platform():
    platform_info = {
        'linux': 'Running on Linux.',
        'win32': 'Running on Windows.',
        'darwin': 'Running on macOS.',
        'java': 'Running on Jython.'
    }
    platform_message = platform_info.get(sys.platform, 'Unknown platform.')
    print(f'{platform_message} (Platform: {sys.platform})')

def check_python_version():
    major, minor, micro = sys.version_info[:3]
    if major >= 3:
        print(f'Running Python {major}.{minor}.{micro} (3.x or higher).')
    else:
        print(f'Running Python {major}.{minor}.{micro} (2.x).')

def get_object_size(obj):
    basic_size = sys.getsizeof(obj)
    detailed_size = basic_size
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple, dict, str, bytes)):
        detailed_size += sum(map(sys.getsizeof, obj))
    print(f'Basic size: {basic_size} bytes, Detailed size: {detailed_size} bytes')

def open_url_if_ok(url):
    try:
        response = requests.get(url)
        if response.status_code == 200:
            webbrowser.open(url, new=1)
        else:
            print(f'The URL returned a {response.status_code} status.')
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        print(f'An error occurred: {e}')

def log(message, level='info', stream=None):
    stream_map = {'info': sys.stdout, 'error': sys.stderr}
    if stream is None:
        stream = stream_map.get(level, sys.stdout)
    if level == 'error':
        message = f'[ERROR] {message}'
    else:
        message = f'[INFO] {message}'
    stream.write(message + '\n')

def redirect_stdout_to_file(filename, message):
    original_stdout = sys.stdout
    try:
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            sys.stdout = f
            print(message)
    finally:
        sys.stdout = original_stdout

        print(f'File specified: {args.file}')
