def discover_bluetooth_devices():
    try:
        nearby_devices = bluetooth.discover_devices()
        device_list = []
        for addr in nearby_devices:
            name = bluetooth.lookup_name(addr)
            device_list.append({'name': name, 'address': addr})
        return device_list
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def fetch_detailed_video_info(query):
    videoSearch = VideosSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = videoSearch.result()
    detailed_info = []
    for video in result['result']:
        video_details = {
            'title': video['title'],
            'views': video['viewCount']['short'],
            'duration': video['duration'],
            'channel': video['channel']['name'],
            'publish_date': video['publishedTime']
        }
        detailed_info.append(video_details)
    return detailed_info

def fetch_detailed_channel_info(query):
    channelSearch = ChannelsSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = channelSearch.result()
    detailed_info = []
    for channel in result['result']:
        channel_details = {
            'title': channel['title'],
            'video_count': channel['videoCount'],
            'subscribers': channel['subscribers'],
            'description': channel['descriptionSnippet'][0]['text']
        }
        detailed_info.append(channel_details)
    return detailed_info

def fetch_detailed_playlist_info(query):
    playlistSearch = PlaylistSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = playlistSearch.result()
    detailed_info = []
    for playlist in result['result']:
        playlist_details = {
            'title': playlist['title'],
            'video_count': playlist['videoCount'],
            'channel': playlist['channel']['name'],
            'total_views': playlist['viewCount']['short']
        }
        detailed_info.append(playlist_details)
    return detailed_info

def batch_fetch_details(query, batch_size):
    videoSearch = VideosSearch(query, limit = batch_size)
    result = videoSearch.result()
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
        details = list(executor.map(fetch_video_details, result['result']))
    return details

def fetch_video_details(video):
    return video['title'], video['viewCount']['short']

def fetch_data_with_auth(url):
    headers = {'User-Agent': 'my-app'}
    auth = HTTPBasicAuth('username', 'password')
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, auth=auth)
    return response.json()

def post_data_handle_error(url, payload):
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload)
    if response.status_code != 200:
        return f'Error: Received status code {response.status_code}'
    return response.json()

def fetch_with_retry(url):
    session = requests.Session()
    retries = Retry(total=3, backoff_factor=0.1, status_forcelist=[ 500, 502, 503, 504 ])
    session.mount('https://', HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retries))
    response = session.get(url)
    return response.text

def fetch_and_log(url, params):
    response = requests.get(url, params=params)
    logging.info(f'Request to {response.url}')
    logging.info(f'Status Code: {response.status_code}')
    logging.info(f'Response: {response.text}')
    return response.json()

def download_resource(url, file_path):
    response = requests.get(url)
    if response.status_code == 200:
        with open(file_path, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(response.content)
    else:
        return f'Failed to download: {response.status_code}'

def fetch_with_dynamic_timeout(url):
    global average_response_time
    start_time = time.time()
    response = requests.get(url, timeout=(average_response_time + 0.5))
    elapsed_time = time.time() - start_time
    average_response_time = (average_response_time + elapsed_time) / 2
    return response.json()

def fetch_with_cache(url):
    if url in cache:
        return cache[url]
    response = requests.get(url)
    cache[url] = response.json()
    return cache[url]

def fetch_with_cookies(url):
    response = session.get(url)
    print(session.cookies.get_dict())
    return response.json()

def fetch_with_custom_ca(url, ca_path):
    response = requests.get(url, verify=ca_path)
    return response.json()

def fetch_and_handle_content(url):
    response = requests.get(url)
    content_type = response.headers.get('content-type')
    if 'application/json' in content_type:
        return response.json()
    elif 'text/html' in content_type:
        return response.text
    else:
        return response.content

    videoSearch = VideosSearch(query, limit = 10)
    for i in range(page - 1):
        videoSearch.next()
    return videoSearch.result()

    results = playlistSearch.result()

    channelSearch = ChannelsSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = channelSearch.result()

def filter_playlists_by_count(query, min_count):
    playlistSearch = PlaylistSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = playlistSearch.result()
    filtered_playlists = [p for p in result['result'] if p['videoCount'] >= min_count]
    return filtered_playlists

def fetch_videos_with_captions(query):
    videoSearch = VideosSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = videoSearch.result()
    videos_with_captions = [v for v in result['result'] if v['hasCaption']]
    return videos_with_captions

def sort_by_like_dislike_ratio(query):
    videoSearch = VideosSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = videoSearch.result()
    sorted_videos = sorted(result['result'], key=lambda x: x['likeCount'] / (x['dislikeCount'] + 1))
    return sorted_videos

def secondary_search_in_videos(query, keyword):
    videoSearch = VideosSearch(query, limit = 10)
    result = videoSearch.result()
    filtered_results = [video for video in result['result'] if keyword.lower() in video['title'].lower()]
    return filtered_results

def bluetooth_connect(bd_addr, port, timeout=10):
    try:
        sock = bluetooth.BluetoothSocket(bluetooth.RFCOMM)
        sock.settimeout(timeout)
        sock.connect((bd_addr, port))
        return 'Successfully connected'
    except (bluetooth.btcommon.BluetoothError, socket.timeout) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def bluetooth_server(max_clients=5):
    server_sock = bluetooth.BluetoothSocket(bluetooth.RFCOMM)
    server_sock.bind(('', 1))
    server_sock.listen(max_clients)
    inputs = [server_sock]
    while True:
        readable, _, _ = select.select(inputs, [], [])
        for s in readable:
            if s is server_sock:
                client_sock, _ = server_sock.accept()
                inputs.append(client_sock)

def authenticated_bluetooth_server():
    server_sock = bluetooth.BluetoothSocket(bluetooth.RFCOMM)
    server_sock.bind(('', 1))
    server_sock.listen(1)
    client_sock, address = server_sock.accept()
    if authenticate_client(address):
        return 'Authenticated'
    else:
        client_sock.close()
        return 'Authentication failed'

def get_weekday_name(year, month, day):
    d = datetime.date(year, month, day)
    weekdays = ['Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday', 'Sunday']
    return weekdays[d.weekday()]

def human_readable_timedelta(start, end):
    delta = end - start
    days, remainder = divmod(delta.total_seconds(), 86400)
    hours, remainder = divmod(remainder, 3600)
    minutes, seconds = divmod(remainder, 60)
    return f'{int(days)} days, {int(hours)} hours, {int(minutes)} minutes, {int(seconds)} seconds'

def parse_date(date_str):
    formats = ['%Y-%m-%d', '%d/%m/%Y', '%m-%d-%Y']
    for fmt in formats:
        try:
            return datetime.datetime.strptime(date_str, fmt)
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return 'Invalid date format'

def iso_with_milliseconds():
    now = datetime.datetime.now()
    return now.isoformat(timespec='milliseconds')

def safe_factorial(x):
    if x < 0:
        return 'Not defined'
    else:
        return math.factorial(x)

def safe_log(x, base=math.e):
    if x <= 0:
        return 'Undefined'
    else:
        return math.log(x, base)

def power(base, exponent):
    if isinstance(exponent, int):
        return math.pow(base, exponent)
    elif isinstance(exponent, float):
        return math.pow(base, exponent)
    else:
        return 'Invalid exponent type'

def custom_sine(x, hyperbolic=False):
    if hyperbolic:
        return math.sinh(x)
    else:
        return math.sin(x)

def case_insensitive_sub(old, new, text):
    return re.sub(old, new, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

def match_pattern(text):
    pattern = re.compile(r'^a[\w]b$')
    return bool(pattern.match(text))

def find_numbers(text):
    return re.findall(r'\b\d{3,4}\b', text)

def calculate_distance(x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2, unit='meters'):
    distance = math.sqrt((x2 - x1) ** 2 + (y2 - y1) ** 2 + (z2 - z1) ** 2)
    if unit == 'kilometers':
        distance /= 1000
    elif unit == 'miles':
        distance *= 0.000621371
    return distance

def calculate_log(x, base=math.e):
    try:
        return math.log(x, base)
    except ValueError:
        return 'Invalid input'

def find_roots(a, b, c):
    discriminant = b ** 2 - 4 * a * c
    if discriminant < 0:
        return 'No real roots'
    root1 = (-b + math.sqrt(discriminant)) / (2 * a)
    root2 = (-b - math.sqrt(discriminant)) / (2 * a)
    return {'root1': root1, 'root2': root2}

def find_angle(ratio, value, unit='degrees'):
    try:
        if ratio == 'tan':
            angle = math.atan(value)
        elif ratio == 'sin':
            angle = math.asin(value)
        elif ratio == 'cos':
            angle = math.acos(value)
        if unit == 'radians':
            return angle
        return math.degrees(angle)
    except ValueError:
        return 'Invalid input'

def get_current_time(timezone='UTC'):
    tz = pytz.timezone(timezone)
    current_time = datetime.datetime.now(tz)
    return {'date': current_time.date(), 'time': current_time.time()}

def days_until_christmas():
    today = datetime.date.today()
    year = today.year if today <= datetime.date(today.year, 12, 25) else today.year + 1
    christmas = datetime.date(year, 12, 25)
    return (christmas - today).days

def time_since_epoch(unit='seconds'):
    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().timestamp()
    if unit == 'milliseconds':
        return timestamp * 1000
    elif unit == 'microseconds':
        return timestamp * 1000000
    return timestamp

def get_weekday(date_str):
    try:
        date_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m-%d')
        weekdays = ['Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday', 'Sunday']
        return weekdays[date_obj.weekday()]
    except ValueError:
        return 'Invalid date string'

def get_age(birthdate):
    today = datetime.date.today()
    age = today.year - birthdate.year
    if (today.month, today.day) < (birthdate.month, birthdate.day):
        age -= 1
    return age

    email = forms.EmailField(required=True)

def allowed_file(filename):
    return '.' in filename and filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS

def upload():
    file = request.files['file']
    if file and allowed_file(file.filename):
        filename = os.path.join('uploads', file.filename)
        file.save(filename)
        return 'File uploaded'
    else:
        abort(400, 'Invalid file type')

class Pausable:
    def __init__(self):
        self._pause_event = asyncio.Event()
        self._pause_event.set()

    async def pause(self):
        self._pause_event.clear()

    async def resume(self):
        self._pause_event.set()

    async def wait_while_paused(self):
        await self._pause_event.wait()

async def print_numbers(pausable):
    for i in range(10):
        print(i)
        await pausable.wait_while_paused()
        await asyncio.sleep(1)

async def download_file(url):
    await asyncio.sleep(2)
    print(f'Downloaded {url}')

async def main():
    urls = ['url1', 'url2', 'url3']
    tasks = [download_file(url) for url in urls]
    await asyncio.gather(*tasks)

def increment():
    global shared_resource
    with resource_lock:

def set_socket_options(host, port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_KEEPALIVE, 1)
        s.bind((host, port))
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Successfully set socket options and bound'

def connect_to_server(host, port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        s.connect((host, port))
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Successfully connected'

def bind_socket(port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    host = socket.gethostname()
    try:
        s.bind((host, port))
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Successfully bound'

def start_listening(port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        s.listen(port)
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Listening'

def accept_connection(s):
    if not s:
        return 'Socket not initialized'
    try:
        c, addr = s.accept()
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return f'Connection from {addr}'

def send_message(s, message):
    if not s or not message:
        return 'Invalid parameters'
    try:
        sent = s.sendall(message.encode('utf-8'))
        if sent == 0:
            raise RuntimeError('Socket connection broken')
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Message sent'

def send_udp_message(host, port, message):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    try:
        s.sendto(message.encode('utf-8'), (host, port))
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Message sent via UDP'

def receive_udp_message(port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    s.bind(('', port))
    try:
        data, addr = s.recvfrom(1024)
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return f'Received message from {addr} : {data.decode('utf-8')}'

def connect_with_timeout(host, port, timeout):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.settimeout(timeout)
    try:
        s.connect((host, port))
    except (socket.timeout, socket.error) as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Successfully connected'

def safe_socket_close(s):
    try:
        if s:
            s.shutdown(socket.SHUT_RDWR)
            s.close()
    except socket.error as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'
    else:
        return 'Socket closed safely'

    for _ in range(5):
        print('Running')

    global n
    with n_lock:
        n += 1

def match_pattern(pattern, string):
    if not isinstance(pattern, str) or not isinstance(string, str):
        return 'Error: Input is not a string'

def run_flake8(file_path):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(['flake8', file_path], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if result.returncode != 0:
            return f'Flake8 errors: {result.stdout}'
        return 'No Flake8 errors'
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def handle_encryption(data):
    try:
        key = Fernet.generate_key()
        with open('key.key', 'wb') as key_file:
            key_file.write(key)
        fernet = Fernet(key)
        encrypted = fernet.encrypt(data)
        return encrypted
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Encryption Error: {e}'

def handle_decryption(encrypted):
    try:
        with open('key.key', 'rb') as key_file:
            key = key_file.read()
        fernet = Fernet(key)
        decrypted = fernet.decrypt(encrypted)
        return decrypted
    except InvalidToken:
        return 'Invalid Token'
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def mock_function():
    m = mock.Mock()
    m.side_effect = [10, 20, 42]
    results = []
    for _ in range(3):
        results.append(m())
    return results

def create_application(app, host, port):
    class StandaloneApplication(gunicorn.app.base.BaseApplication):
        def init(self, *args, **kwargs):
            return {'bind': f'{host}:{port}'}

        def load(self):
            return app
    return StandaloneApplication()

def run_flake8():
    try:
        style_guide = flake8.get_style_guide(ignore=['E501', 'W605'])
        report = style_guide.check_files(['./project'])
        if report.total_errors > 0:
            return f'Total errors: {report.total_errors}'
        return 'No errors found'
    except Exception as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def encrypt_decrypt():
    try:
        key = Fernet.generate_key()
        cipher_suite = Fernet(key)
        text = cipher_suite.encrypt(b'Hello World')
        decrypted_text = cipher_suite.decrypt(text)
        return decrypted_text.decode('utf-8')
    except InvalidToken as e:
        return f'Error: {e}'

def mock_api():
    api_call = Mock()
    api_call.get.return_value.status_code = 200
    api_call.get.return_value.json.return_value = {'key': 'value'}
    response = api_call.get()
    if response.status_code == 200:
        return response.json()
    return 'API Error'

class MyApplication(BaseApplication):
    def __init__(self, app, options=None):
        self.options = options or {}
        self.application = app
        super(MyApplication, self).__init__()

    def load_config(self):
        for key, value in self.options.items():
            self.cfg.set(key.lower(), value)
    
    def load(self):
        return self.application

def apply_sequence_of_filters(image_path, filters):
    try:
        image = Image.open(image_path)
        for filter_type in filters:
            if hasattr(ImageFilter, filter_type):
                image = image.filter(getattr(ImageFilter, filter_type))
        image.save('sequentially_filtered_example.jpg')
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        print(f'File not found: {e}')

def annotate_image(image_path, annotations):
    try:
        image = Image.open(image_path)
        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        font = ImageFont.load_default()
        for position, text in annotations.items():
            draw.text(position, text, font=font, fill=(255, 255, 255))
        image.save('annotated_example.jpg')
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        print(f'File not found: {e}')

def overlay_images_with_transparency(base_image_path, overlay_image_path, n_times):
    try:
        base_image = Image.open(base_image_path)
        overlay_image = Image.open(overlay_image_path)
        for i in range(n_times):
            temp_overlay = overlay_image.copy()
            temp_overlay.putalpha(255 - i*25)
            base_image.paste(temp_overlay, (i*50, i*50), temp_overlay)
        base_image.save('varying_transparency_overlay.jpg')
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        print(f'File not found: {e}')

def rotate_images_by_angles(image_paths, angles):
    try:
        for i, (image_path, angle) in enumerate(zip(image_paths, angles)):
            image = Image.open(image_path)
            image = image.rotate(angle)
            image.save(f'rotated_by_{angle}_degrees_{i}.jpg')
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        print(f'File not found: {e}')

def detect_circles(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    circles = cv2.HoughCircles(gray, cv2.HOUGH_GRADIENT, 1, minDist=50, param1=50, param2=30)
    if circles is not None:
        circles = np.uint16(np.around(circles))
        for i in circles[0,:]:
            cv2.circle(image, (i[0], i[1]), i[2], (0, 255, 0), 2)
    cv2.imshow('Detected Circles', image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def stitch_images(image_paths):
    images = [cv2.imread(image_path) for image_path in image_paths]
    stitcher = cv2.Stitcher_create()
    status, panorama = stitcher.stitch(images)
    if status == cv2.Stitcher_OK:
        cv2.imshow('Panorama', panorama)
        cv2.waitKey(0)
        cv2.destroyAllWindows()
    else:
        print('Error during stitching')

def detect_faces(image_path):
    face_cascade = cv2.CascadeClassifier(cv2.data.haarcascades + 'haarcascade_frontalface_default.xml')
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    faces = face_cascade.detectMultiScale(gray, 1.3, 5)
    for (x, y, w, h) in faces:
        cv2.rectangle(image, (x, y), (x+w, y+h), (255, 0, 0), 2)
    cv2.imshow('Face Detection', image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def apply_gaussian_blur(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(image, (15, 15), 0)
    cv2.imshow('Gaussian Blurred', blurred)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def detect_sift_features(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    sift = cv2.SIFT_create()
    keypoints, descriptors = sift.detectAndCompute(gray, None)
    img_with_keypoints = cv2.drawKeypoints(gray, keypoints, image)
    cv2.imshow('SIFT KeyPoints', img_with_keypoints)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def calculate_histogram(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    for i, color in enumerate(['b', 'g', 'r']):
        histogram, bin_edges = np.histogram(
            image[:, :, i], bins=256, range=(0, 255)
        )
        plt.plot(bin_edges[0:-1], histogram, color=color)
    plt.title('Color Histogram')
    plt.show()

def extract_roi(image_path, x, y, w, h):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    roi = image[y:y+h, x:x+w]
    cv2.imshow('Region of Interest', roi)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def overlay_text(image_path, text, position):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    font = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    scale = 1
    color = (255, 255, 255)
    thickness = 2
    cv2.putText(image, text, position, font, scale, color, thickness)
    cv2.imshow('Text Overlay', image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def compare_images(image_path1, image_path2):
    image1 = cv2.imread(image_path1, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    image2 = cv2.imread(image_path2, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    similarity_index, _ = ssim(image1, image2, full=True)
    print(f'Structural Similarity Index: {similarity_index}')

def apply_gaussian_blur(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    blurred_image = cv2.GaussianBlur(image, (15, 15), 0)
    cv2.imshow('Original Image', image)
    cv2.imshow('Blurred Image', blurred_image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def draw_shapes(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    cv2.circle(image, (150, 150), 100, (0, 0, 255), 5)
    cv2.rectangle(image, (300, 300), (500, 500), (255, 0, 0), 5)
    cv2.line(image, (0, 0), (image.shape[1], image.shape[0]), (0, 255, 0), 5)
    cv2.imshow('Shapes on Image', image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def capture_video():
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(0)
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        cv2.imshow('Video Capture', frame)
        if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord('q'):
            break
    cap.release()
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def generate_image_pyramid(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    layer = image.copy()
    for i in range(4):
        cv2.imshow(f'Layer {i}', layer)
        layer = cv2.pyrDown(layer)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def apply_canny_edge_detection(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    edges = cv2.Canny(gray, 50, 150)
    cv2.imshow('Canny Edge Detection', edges)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def blend_images(image1_path, image2_path):
    img1 = cv2.imread(image1_path)
    img2 = cv2.imread(image2_path)
    alpha = 0.3
    blended = cv2.addWeighted(img1, alpha, img2, 1-alpha, 0)
    cv2.imshow('Blended Image', blended)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def track_object_by_color():
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(0)
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        lower_range = np.array([110, 50, 50])
        upper_range = np.array([130, 255, 255])
        mask = cv2.inRange(hsv, lower_range, upper_range)
        result = cv2.bitwise_and(frame, frame, mask=mask)
        cv2.imshow('Object Tracking', result)
        if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord('q'):
            break
    cap.release()
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def create_panorama(image1_path, image2_path):
    img1 = cv2.imread(image1_path)
    img2 = cv2.imread(image2_path)
    panorama = np.hstack((img1, img2))
    cv2.imshow('Panorama', panorama)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def video_to_gif(video_path, gif_path):
    frames = []
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        frame_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        frames.append(frame_rgb)
    cap.release()
    imageio.mimsave(gif_path, frames, fps=24)

def segment_image_with_grabcut(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    mask = np.zeros(image.shape[:2], np.uint8)
    bgd_model = np.zeros((1, 65), np.float64)
    fgd_model = np.zeros((1, 65), np.float64)
    rect = (10, 10, image.shape[1]-10, image.shape[0]-10)
    cv2.grabCut(image, mask, rect, bgd_model, fgd_model, 5, cv2.GC_INIT_WITH_RECT)
    mask2 = np.where((mask==2)|(mask==0), 0, 1).astype('uint8')
    segmented = image * mask2[:, :, np.newaxis]
    cv2.imshow('Segmented Image', segmented)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def detect_faces(image_path):
    face_cascade = cv2.CascadeClassifier(cv2.data.haarcascades + 'haarcascade_frontalface_default.xml')
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    gray_image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    faces = face_cascade.detectMultiScale(gray_image, scaleFactor=1.1, minNeighbors=5)
    for (x, y, w, h) in faces:
        cv2.rectangle(image, (x, y), (x+w, y+h), (255, 0, 0), 2)
    cv2.imshow('Detected Faces', image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def apply_canny(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    edges = cv2.Canny(image, 100, 200)
    cv2.imshow('Edges', edges)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def ocr_text(image_path):
    pytesseract.pytesseract.tesseract_cmd = r'C:\Program Files\Tesseract-OCR\tesseract.exe'
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    text = pytesseract.image_to_string(image)
    print(f'Text extracted from image: {text}')

def match_features(image1_path, image2_path):
    img1 = cv2.imread(image1_path, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    img2 = cv2.imread(image2_path, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    sift = cv2.SIFT_create()
    kp1, des1 = sift.detectAndCompute(img1, None)
    kp2, des2 = sift.detectAndCompute(img2, None)
    bf = cv2.BFMatcher()
    matches = bf.knnMatch(des1, des2, k=2)
    good_matches = []
    for m, n in matches:
        if m.distance < 0.75 * n.distance:
            good_matches.append([m])
    img_matches = cv2.drawMatchesKnn(img1, kp1, img2, kp2, good_matches, None, flags=cv2.DrawMatchesFlags_NOT_DRAW_SINGLE_POINTS)
    cv2.imshow('Feature Matching', img_matches)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

def perform_lsi(corpus, dictionary, num_topics=5):
    tfidf = models.TfidfModel(corpus)
    corpus_tfidf = tfidf[corpus]
    lsi = models.LsiModel(corpus_tfidf, id2word=dictionary, num_topics=num_topics)
    return lsi

def find_similar_docs(model_path, target_doc):
    model = Doc2Vec.load(model_path)
    inferred_vector = model.infer_vector(target_doc.words)
    similar_docs = model.docvecs.most_similar([inferred_vector], topn=5)
    return similar_docs

def text_to_bow(texts):
    dictionary = corpora.Dictionary(texts)
    corpus = [dictionary.doc2bow(text) for text in texts]
    return corpus, dictionary

def train_word2vec(corpus, size=100, window=5, min_count=1):
    model = Word2Vec(corpus, size=size, window=window, min_count=min_count, workers=4)
    model.save('word2vec.model')
    return model

def update_word2vec_model(model_path, new_data):
    model = Word2Vec.load(model_path)
    model.build_vocab(new_data, update=True)
    model.train(new_data, total_examples=model.corpus_count, epochs=model.epochs)
    model.save('updated_word2vec.model')

def generate_summary(text, num_sentences=5):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    sentences = blob.sentences
    word_freq = Counter(blob.words)
    ranking = {}
    for i, sentence in enumerate(sentences):
        ranking[i] = 0
        for word in sentence.words:
            if word.lower() in word_freq:
                ranking[i] += word_freq[word.lower()]
    sorted_ranking = sorted(ranking.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:num_sentences]
    summary = [str(sentences[i[0]]) for i in sorted_ranking]
    return ' '.join(summary)

def find_syn_ant(word):
    syns = [syn.lemmas()[0].name() for syn in wordnet.synsets(word)]
    ants = []
    for syn in wordnet.synsets(word):
        for lemma in syn.lemmas():
            if lemma.antonyms():
                ants.append(lemma.antonyms()[0].name())
    return syns, ants

def convert_form(word, to_plural=True):
    w = Word(word)
    if to_plural:
        return w.pluralize()
    else:
        return w.singularize()

def extract_keywords(text, n=5):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    sentences = [str(sentence) for sentence in blob.sentences]
    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer()
    tfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(sentences)
    feature_names = vectorizer.get_feature_names()
    dense = tfidf_matrix.todense()
    dense_list = dense.tolist()
    keyword_list = []
    for i in range(len(dense_list)):
        sentence_keywords = [feature_names[index] for index in np.argsort(dense_list[i])[::-1][:n]]
        keyword_list.append(sentence_keywords)
    return keyword_list

def get_ngrams(text, n):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    ngrams = blob.ngrams(n=n)
    ngram_freq = Counter([' '.join(gram) for gram in ngrams])
    return ngram_freq

def summarize_text(text, num_sentences=5):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    words = blob.words.lower()
    word_freq = Counter(words)
    sentences = blob.sentences
    ranked_sentences = sorted(sentences, key=lambda s: sum(word_freq[w.lower()] for w in s.words), reverse=True)
    summary = ' '.join(str(s) for s in ranked_sentences[:num_sentences])
    return summary

def flesch_reading_ease(text):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    num_sentences = len(blob.sentences)
    num_words = len(blob.words)
    num_syllables = sum(1 for word in blob.words for vowel in 'aeiouAEIOU' if vowel in word)
    score = 206.835 - 1.015 * (num_words / num_sentences) - 84.6 * (num_syllables / num_words)
    return score

class Particle:
    def __init__(self, x, y):
        self.x = x
        self.y = y
        self.vx = randint(-5, 5)
        self.vy = randint(-5, 5)

    def update(self, dt):
        self.x += self.vx * dt
        self.y += self.vy * dt

def update(dt):
    for p in particles:
        p.update(dt)

def on_draw():
    window.clear()
    for p in particles:
        pyglet.graphics.draw(1, pyglet.gl.GL_POINTS, ('v2f', [p.x, p.y]))

def update(dt):
    global x, y, vx, vy
    vy += gravity * dt
    x += vx * dt
    y += vy * dt
    if y < 0:
        y = 0
        vy = -vy * 0.7

def sort_by_sentiment(reviews):

def summarize_article(article_text):
    blob = TextBlob(article_text)
    paragraphs = article_text.split('\n\n')
    summary = []
    for para in paragraphs:
        blob = TextBlob(para)
        summary.append(blob.sentences[0])
    return ' '.join(str(sentence) for sentence in summary)

def chatbot_response(text):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    if blob.sentiment.polarity > 0.5:
        return 'I'm glad you're happy!'
    elif blob.sentiment.polarity < -0.5:
        return 'I'm sorry you're feeling this way.'
    else:
        return 'I see. How can I assist you further?'

def detect_and_translate(text):
    blob = TextBlob(text)
    detected_lang = blob.detect_language()
    if detected_lang != 'en':
        translated_text = blob.translate(to='en')
        return translated_text, detected_lang
    return text, detected_lang

def recognize_license_plate(image_path):
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    grey = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(grey, (5,5), 0)
    _, thresh = cv2.threshold(blur, 0, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY + cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    text = pytesseract.image_to_string(Image.fromarray(thresh))
    return text

def redact_information(text):
    ssn_pattern = r'\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}'
    redacted_text = re.sub(ssn_pattern, 'XXX-XX-XXXX', text)
    return redacted_text

class TestMyFunction(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_double_of_two(self):
        self.assertEqual(my_function(2), 4)

    def test_double_of_five(self):
        self.assertEqual(my_function(5), 10)

def lcm(x, y):
    """
    This function takes two integers and returns their least common multiple.
    Parameters:
    x (int): first integer
    y (int): second integer
    Returns:
    int: Least Common Multiple (LCM) of x and y
    """
    # Find the greater number between x and y
    if x > y:
        greater = x
    else:
        greater = y

    # Continue looping until a common multiple is found
    while True:
        if greater % x == 0 and greater % y == 0:
            lcm = greater
            break
        greater += 1

    return lcm

def print_reverse(my_list):
    for i in reversed(my_list):
        print(i)

def sort_array_by_column(arr, col_index):
    return sorted(arr, key=lambda x: x[col_index])

def reverse_list(l):
    reversed_list = l[::-1]
    return reversed_list

result = reverse_list(my_list)
print(result)

def increment_visitor_count():
    """This method will be called every time a new visitor loads the webpage."""
    global visitors
    visitors += 1

def get_visitor_count():
    """This function returns the current number of visitors to the website."""
    return visitors

def reset_visitor_count():
    """Resets the visitor count to 0."""
    global visitors
    visitors = 0

def find_min_max(numbers):
    max_value = numbers[0]
    min_value = numbers[0]

    for number in numbers:
        if number > max_value:
            max_value = number
        elif number < min_value:
            min_value = number

    print(f"Maximum number: {max_value}")
    print(f"Minimum number: {min_value}")

def is_valid_email(email):
    if "@" in email:
        username, domain = email.split("@")
        if "." in domain:
            # Check if the domain has any special character(except ".")
            if any(char.isdigit() or char.isalpha() or char == '.' for char in domain):
                return True
    return False

class TestEmailValidator(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_valid_emails(self):
        emails = ["test@example.com", "test.email@domain.uk", "test.email+alex@domain.co.jp"]
        for email in emails:
            self.assertTrue(is_valid_email(email))

    def test_invalid_emails(self):
        emails = ["plainaddress", "@missingusername.com", "missingatsign.com"]
        for email in emails:
            self.assertFalse(is_valid_email(email))

    def test_invalid_domains(self):
        emails = ["username@.com", "username@.a"]
        for email in emails:
            self.assertFalse(is_valid_email(email))

    def test_invalid_chars_in_domain(self):
        emails = ["username@%*.com"]
        for email in emails:
            self.assertFalse(is_valid_email(email))

def read_csv_file(file_path):
    if os.path.exists(file_path):
        df = pd.read_csv(file_path)
        return df
    else:
        return 'File not found'

def load_keras_model(model_path):
    if os.path.exists(model_path):
        try:
            model = load_model(model_path)
        except Exception as e:
            return f'Error: {e}'
        else:
            return 'Model loaded successfully'
    else:
        return 'Model path does not exist'

def forward_pass(x, w, b):
    return torch.matmul(x, w) + b

def is_even(num):

        return True 
    else: 
        return False

def quick_sort(arr):
    """Sort a list of numbers in ascending order using the Quick-Sort algorithm"""
    if len(arr) == 0:
        return []

    pivot = arr[0]
    left_arr = [x for x in arr if x < pivot]
    right_arr = [x for x in arr if x > pivot]
    return quick_sort(left_arr) + [pivot] + quick_sort(right_arr)

def draw_word(word):
    art = """
                 __
           _   /  \\
           \\_/\_/\_/
           /    |
           \___/
          \\\\_//
           \/ \/
            \/
        """
    for letter in word:
        art += "  " + letter
    print(art)

def list_to_string(input_list, separator):

def calc_mean(arr):
    sum = 0
    for num in arr:
        sum += num
    return sum / len(arr)

def remove_value(nums, val):
    res = []
    for num in nums:
        if num != val:
            res.append(num)
    return res

def generate_fibonacci_numbers(n):
   if n==1:
       return [0]
   elif n==2:
       return [0,1]
   else:
       fib_list = [0,1]
       for i in range(2,n):
           fib_list.append(fib_list[i-1]+fib_list[i-2])
       return fib_list

def remove_duplicates(list): 
      
    unique_list = [] 
      
    for x in list: 
        if x not in unique_list: 
            unique_list.append(x) 
    return unique_list 
  
list = [2, 3, 2, 4, 5, 5, 6] 
print ("Original List: ", list) 
print ("Unique List: ", remove_duplicates(list)) 

Output: 
Original List: [2, 3, 2, 4, 5, 5, 6]
Unique List: [2, 3, 4, 5, 6]

def median(arr):
  sortedArr = sorted(arr)
  length = len(arr)
  if length % 2 == 0:
    return ((sortedArr[length//2-1] + sortedArr[length//2])/2)
  else:
    return sortedArr[length//2]

def sum_of_list(my_list):
  sum = 0
  for number in my_list:
    sum = sum + number
  return sum

def factorial(num):
    """
    Calculates factorial of a given number.
    """
    # Base case
    if num == 0:
        return 1
    # Recursion
    else:
        return num * factorial(num-1)

def first_non_repeating_char(input_str): 
 
    char_count = {}
 
    for ch in input_str: 
 
        if ch in char_count: 
 
            char_count[ch] += 1
 
        else: 
 
            char_count[ch] = 1
 
    for ch in input_str: 
 
        if char_count[ch] == 1: 
            return ch 
 
    return None

def reverse_string(input_str): 
    new_string = "" 
    for char in input_str: 
        new_string = char + new_string
  
    return new_string 
  
input_str = "Hello world!"

print(reverse_string(input_str))

 return sorted(word1) == sorted(word2)

def max_values(d):
 return {key: max(d.values()) for key in d}

def count_chars(string):
 char_occurrences = {}
 for char in string:
  if char not in char_occurrences:
   char_occurrences[char] = 1
  else:
   char_occurrences[char] += 1
 return char_occurrences

def generate_powerset(list):
 powerset = []
 for i in range(2**len(list)):
  subset = [list[bit] for bit in range(len(list)) if is_bit_set(i, bit)]
  powerset.append(subset)
 return powerset

def gcd(a, b):
     while b != 0:
        a, b = b, a % b
     return a

def common_elements(list1, list2):
    common_ele = []   
    for i in list1:
        for j in list2:
            if i==j:
                common_ele.append(i)
    return common_ele

def contains_uppercase(text):
  for char in text:
    if char.isupper():
      return True
  return False
